## ROLL-5-1A — Staging Restore Drill Evidence & Stage-1 GO Clearance (2026-07-10)

Branch `feature/roll-5-1a-staging-restore-drill-evidence-stage-1-go-clearance` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, baseline `roll-5-1-...-go` @ `0fda7e5`; do NOT target main). Gap map: `docs/sprints/roll-5-1a-existing-restore-drill-gap-map.md`; sprint doc `docs/sprints/roll-5-1a-staging-restore-drill-evidence-stage-1-go-clearance.md`; runbook `docs/runbooks/roll-5-backup-restore-drill-runbook.md`; evidence template `docs/evidence/rollout/restore-drill-template.md`; cursor rule `.cursor/rules/70-roll-5-1a-restore-drill-evidence.mdc`. **Clears the restore-drill WATCH from ROLL-5-1 so Stage-1 readiness can move to GO** — without faking GO if no real staging restore was performed. Reuses ROLL-5-1, MON-1, NSF-9/10, CICD-CTRL-1, the ENT-11 deploy runner, and the ENT-12 rehearsal harness — does not rebuild any. **No migration, no new permission, no new route, no seeder/role change, no runtime driver change; ledger-only inventory, branch isolation, KTP/NIK/secrets/raw-logs privacy preserved.** **Scope A:** canonical versioned evidence JSON `storage/app/readiness/restore-drills/latest.json` (schema_version 1) + `config/rollout_readiness.php` `restore_drill` block (schema version, canonical path, verification keys, forbidden environments, safe target markers) and canonical-only `paths.restore_drill_evidence` (deliberately does NOT parse the ENT-12 `restore-rehearsal.json` — different schema). **Scope B:** read-only `App\Services\Foundation\RestoreDrillEvidenceService` — locate → raw secret/PII scan → schema-validate → `production_overwrite === false` (else UNSAFE FAIL) → non-production `environment` (else UNSAFE FAIL) → decision/verification FAIL → source-backup size/local-existence → staleness; returns GO/WATCH/FAIL + sanitized whitelisted details + remediation. Missing ⇒ WATCH; unsafe ⇒ FAIL; stale/incomplete ⇒ WATCH; `--create-template` placeholder (drill_id contains `TEMPLATE`) ⇒ WATCH not FAIL. Masks all free text via `SensitiveValueMasker`; never echoes a raw secret/KTP/NIK. **Scope C:** `php artisan rollout:restore-drill-evidence` (`--json`/`--strict`/`--fail-on-warning`/`--path=`/`--create-template`) — VALIDATES evidence only, never restores; `--strict` exits 1 on FAIL only (WATCH exits 0). **Scope D:** runbook now has execution-ready Option 1/2/3 + a pre-restore safety checklist; new fail-fast `scripts/rollout-restore-drill.sh` restores the latest verified backup into a **disposable** `<db>_restore_drill_<stamp>` DB (guarded ≠ production, name must contain a safe marker; env not production/pilot/live), read-only counts (no patient rows), drops **only** the disposable DB, writes canonical evidence (password never echoed); never `migrate:fresh`/`db:wipe`/over-production. **Scope E:** `FiveBranchRolloutReadinessService::restoreDrillSignal()` delegates to the evidence service; new pure `decideStage(baseStatus, branchStatus)` + `baseReadinessStatus()` make each stage's status = worst(shared base readiness incl. restore drill, that stage's branch-count gate) — **Stage-1 clears to GO independently of Stage-3's 5-branch count**; Stage-3 stays WATCH until 5 RME branches; stage entries add `branch_status` + `base_status`. **Scope F:** UI `/foundation/rollout/five-branch-readiness` adds a restore-drill evidence card + a Stage-1 clearance panel (shows later stages still WATCH); read-only, sanitized, never runs a restore. **Scope G/H:** durable rules (restore evidence required before Stage-1 GO; drill never targets production; sanitized; missing⇒WATCH; unsafe⇒FAIL; Stage-1 independent of Stage-3; Stage-3 WATCH until 5 branches; UI read-only; command validates-not-restores). Tests: new `tests/Feature/Foundation/{RestoreDrillEvidenceServiceTest(10),RolloutRestoreDrillEvidenceCommandTest(5),RolloutStageOneClearanceTest(6),RolloutRestoreDrillUiTest(5)}`; existing ROLL-5 stage test repinned `status`→`branch_status`. GO tag on merge: `roll-5-1a-staging-restore-drill-evidence-stage-1-go-clearance-go`. Deploy note: **no migration/seed needed**; on VPS run `rollout:restore-drill-evidence` + `rollout:five-branch-readiness --stage=1|2|3` + `--include-audits --strict` + `foundation:monitoring-observability-check --include-audits --strict` + `inventory:procurement-workflow-audit --strict` + `rme:doctor-performance-access-audit --strict` + `foundation:ci-runtime-control-check --strict`; optionally run `scripts/rollout-restore-drill.sh` only if a disposable/staging DB is safe (never over production) — else Stage-1 restore evidence stays WATCH honestly.

## FIX-PO-MULTI-VENDOR — Item-Level Vendor Selection, Estimated Arrival Date & Supplier PDF (2026-07-10)

Branch `feature/fix-po-multi-vendor-item-estimated-arrival-supplier-pdf` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/fix-po-multi-vendor-item-estimated-arrival-supplier-pdf.md`; cursor rule `.cursor/rules/71-po-multi-vendor-item-supplier-estimated-arrival-pdf.mdc`. Makes **supplier canonical at the PO item level** so one PO can carry items from several suppliers, adds a per-item **estimated arrival date**, and produces a **vendor-scoped supplier PDF**. Procurement flow `PR → PO → GR → movement PURCHASE` preserved; inventory ledger-only; branch isolation, authorization, batch tracking, approval workflow unchanged; KTP/NIK not applicable. **Migration (additive only — `migrate`, never `migrate:fresh`/`db:wipe`):** `2026_07_10_100001_add_supplier_and_estimated_arrival_to_trx_purchase_order_items_table` adds nullable indexed `trx_purchase_order_items.supplier_id` (FK `inv_suppliers`, nullOnDelete) + `estimated_arrival_date` (date) + indexes `trx_po_items_supplier_id_index` / `trx_po_items_order_supplier_index`; **deterministic chunked PG+SQLite-safe backfill** of legacy item.supplier_id ← header supplier_id and item.estimated_arrival_date ← header expected_delivery_date. **Legacy header `trx_purchase_orders.supplier_id`/`supplier_snapshot_name` is NOT dropped — kept as a DEPRECATED, DERIVED compatibility snapshot** (sole distinct item supplier, or NULL for multi-vendor). Key rules: (1) supplier is per item, never a canonical header input; (2) every item requires supplier + estimated arrival (`estimated_arrival_date >= order_date`, enforced in `ValidatesPurchaseOrderInput` + service); (3) prices/per-supplier subtotals/grand total server-side (`PurchaseOrder::supplierGroups()`/`subtotalForSupplier()`/`totalAmount()`); (4) supplier PDF `GET inventory/purchase-orders/{purchaseOrder}/supplier/{supplier}/pdf` (`inventory.purchase-orders.supplier-pdf`, reuses existing `view` policy — **no new permission**) is vendor-scoped via `PurchaseOrderService::buildSupplierPdfData()` with server-side supplier **membership** + branch checks — never leaks another supplier's items/prices/existence; dompdf table-based blade `resources/views/inventory/purchase-orders/supplier-pdf.blade.php`; (5) GR references the PO item explicitly and the `PURCHASE` movement records the **item's** supplier (`poItem.supplier_id ?? header.supplier_id`) — void reversal reuses the original movement's supplier; PO completion computed across all items. Service `applyItemDefaults()` = documented compatibility layer (a header `supplier_id` defaults blank item suppliers, order date defaults blank arrival, for legacy/programmatic callers; the HTTP FormRequest still requires per-item supplier + arrival so the UI/API boundary is never weakened). `submit`/`approve`/`send` now assert **every item** supplier is active (header may be null). UI: `_form.blade.php` item-driven (per-item supplier select + estimated arrival + Alpine dynamic per-vendor summary; header supplier select removed); `show.blade.php` groups items by supplier with per-supplier subtotal, grand total, and per-supplier PDF download buttons. Tests: new `tests/Feature/Inventory/PurchaseOrderMultiVendorTest.php` + `PurchaseOrderSupplierPdfTest.php`; existing PO/GR fixtures updated for per-item supplier + arrival; schema test asserts the new columns. GO tag on merge: `fix-po-multi-vendor-item-estimated-arrival-supplier-pdf-go`. Deploy note: run `php artisan migrate --force` on VPS (additive column + backfill; no seed/permission change).

## LAB-WORKFLOW-V2 — Cabang Pickup, Courier Transport, Internal/External Production, QC, Delivery Proof & Legacy Inactivation (2026-07-10)

Dikirim sebagai **5 PR berfase, masing-masing deploy VPS + GO tag sendiri** (presisi FIX-PRE-68-45): Phase 1 `#238`→`9116274` (`lab-workflow-v2-phase-1-foundation-state-machine-legacy-gate-go`), Phase 2 `#239`→`21f86f3` (`...-phase-2-cabang-request-courier-pickup-receive-go`), Phase 3 `#240`→`ee0c800` (`...-phase-3-analysis-production-qc-external-lab-go`), Phase 4 `#241`→`8fd2ba0` (`...-phase-4-delivery-proof-gates-go`), Phase 5 = notifikasi in-app + label legacy + KPI + rules + aktivasi. Base semua fase: `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report` (JANGAN target main).

**Aturan permanen (canonical, mirror `.cursor/rules/72-lab-workflow-v2.mdc`):**
1. **Lab Workflow V2 canonical untuk order lab baru.** Engine dipilih HANYA oleh `LabWorkflowResolver` via flag `lab.workflow_v2` (env `FEATURE_LAB_WORKFLOW_V2`, default OFF di config; DIAKTIFKAN di VPS pilot saat aktivasi Phase 5). `trx_lab_orders.workflow_version`: LEGACY=1 (NULL=LEGACY), V2=2.
2. **Workflow lama tidak dihapus — inactive + read-only untuk order baru** saat V2 aktif: `LabOrderService::create` ditolak server-side; update/cancel legacy menolak order V2; UI legacy diberi badge "Legacy Workflow — Inactive for New Orders". Retirement penuh butuh sprint khusus.
3. **Semua transisi V2 lewat `LabWorkflowStateMachine::transition()`** (matrix `LabWorkflowState::matrix()`, 42 status; permission map multi-nilai `config/lab_workflow.php` via `canAny`; `lockForUpdate`; idempotent same-state no-op; append-only `trx_lab_order_status_logs` + `sys_audit_logs`, metadata scalar-only tanpa PII/binary). Update status langsung dari request DILARANG.
4. **Alur**: DRAFT → (SPK photo + model photo WAJIB) → WAITING_PICKUP → pickup kurir (`trx_lab_pickup_tasks`, claim first-committed-wins, foto pickup wajib) → IN_TRANSIT_TO_LAB → RECEIVED_AT_LAB (hanya `manage_lab_orders` — kurir tak bisa konfirmasi sendiri) → MODEL_REGISTERED → MODEL_ANALYSIS_PENDING → keputusan analisa wajib beralasan (`trx_lab_model_analyses`, append-only) → INTERNAL (assign teknisi → step 1–4 berurutan ketat → QC) ATAU EXTERNAL (`mst_external_labs` + `trx_lab_external_dispatches`, sent/in-progress/returned/result-review; reject = putaran dispatch BARU) → MODEL_DONE → delivery (`trx_lab_delivery_tasks`).
5. **QC**: gagal wajib alasan + target rework eksplisit (default STEP_2); rework membuka kembali step target..4 + reaktivasi assignment; nomor revisi dari hitungan log QC_FAILED. **SEGREGATION OF DUTY**: teknisi produsen tidak boleh pass/fail QC-nya sendiri (baru di V2, tidak ada di legacy).
6. **Delivery proof (aturan owner, 3 lapis server-side)**: sebelum IN_TRANSIT_TO_BRANCH wajib foto serah terima lab→kurir + TTD kurir; DELIVERED wajib nama + TTD penerima + foto lokasi/bukti. FormRequest keduanya/ketiganya required + service simpan atomik & re-assert + matrix tanpa edge bypass. Disabled button bukan security.
7. **Bukti privat bertipe** `trx_lab_workflow_evidence` (7 tipe eksplisit, sha256, soft-delete only — bukti audit tidak boleh hard-delete) di disk privat `local`; serve hanya via `lab-workflow-evidence.show` + `LabWorkflowEvidencePolicy` (lab/kurir semua; aktor cabang = cabangnya). Signature = file PNG divalidasi magic bytes (bukan base64 di DB). Validasi bytes riil `getimagesizefromstring` (anti MIME-spoof).
8. **Branch**: transisi aktor-cabang saja yang branch-equality-scoped (`branch_scoped_transitions`); kurir/lab lintas-cabang by design, dijaga kepemilikan task. Workspace cabang selalu `BranchContext` — jangan percaya branch_id request.
9. **Notifikasi = in-app database notifications saja** (`LabWorkflowNotificationService`, halaman `/notifications`, self-scoped) — TIDAK ada auto-WhatsApp; kegagalan notifikasi di-log dan tidak pernah membatalkan transisi.
10. **RME → LabCaseCandidate → convert tetap manual review**; saat V2 aktif konversi menghasilkan order V2 DRAFT (`createV2Draft`); kegagalan lab TIDAK me-rollback payment RME.
11. Report produksi legacy membaca `trx_lab_order_assignments.status='DONE'` — semantik status assignment V2 dipertahankan identik. Permissions baru hanya Phase 2: `create_lab_branch_requests` (Perawat/Admin Klinik/Admin Lab), `manage_lab_pickups` (Courier/Admin Lab).
12. **Test-infra**: CLI lokal tanpa GD — pakai `fakeEvidencePhoto()` (PNG bytes riil) di `tests/Pest.php`, JANGAN `UploadedFile::fake()->image()`. Suite: `tests/Feature/LabWorkflow/` (foundation/branch-request/pickup/analysis-production/external/delivery-proof).

(6) Workflow V2 tidak berubah: `workflow_version=2`, legacy create tetap blocked, pickup idempotent, redirect sukses ke `lab-workflow-requests.show`. (2) Audit permission WAJIB effective (`$user->can()`/`getAllPermissions()`/`getRoleNames()`) — direct-only `whereHas('permissions')` melewatkan role permission + `Gate::before`. (4) Operator Lab Request wajib `users.branch_id` → Cabang RME aktif; MAIN/non-RME dilarang. (5) Mapping produksi = evidence-based/owner-confirmed, transaksional + lock + validasi aktif+RME + before/after; tidak boleh auto-assign. (6) Ops data repair tanpa code change tidak butuh GO tag/deploy — cukup evidence doc `docs/operations/lab-request-operator-rme-branch-mapping-2026-07-10.md`.

Branch `feature/rme-branch-sun4-perawat-online-context` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`; do NOT target main). Doc: `docs/sprints/rme-branch-sun4-perawat-online-context.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/73-rme-branch-sun4-perawat-online-context.mdc`. **Registry kode cabang canonical: TKM1=Cabang Telkomas, LDK2=Cabang Landak, ATG3=Cabang Antang, SUN4=Cabang Sunu** — jangan reuse/renumber; resolve by `code`, bukan ID lintas environment. **[SUPERSEDED 2026-08-31 oleh REVISION-TELKOMAS-BRANCH-CODE-TKM1-TO-TLK1-1: kode canonical Cabang Telkomas sekarang `TLK1`; `TKM1` DEPRECATED — diterima sebagai alias historis, tidak pernah lagi di-generate.]** **[SUPERSEDED 2026-09-02 oleh REVISION-SUNU-BRANCH-CODE-SUN4-TO-SPN4-1: kode canonical Cabang Sunu sekarang `SPN4`; `SUN4` DEPRECATED — diterima sebagai alias historis, tidak pernah lagi di-generate. Nama sprint `RME-BRANCH-SUN4` adalah identifier historis dan TIDAK di-rename.]** **No migration, no permission change.** (1) **SUN4** ditambahkan idempotent via `Database\Seeders\RmeBranchSeeder` (`firstOrNew` by unique `code` incl. soft-deleted; row baru aktif+RME-enabled+`is_inventory_enabled=false`; existing TKM1/LDK2/ATG3 TIDAK di-rename/reconfigure; SUN4 di-restore+re-enable bila trashed/disabled; MAIN tidak disentuh; rollback tidak menghapus cabang bertransaksi) — terdaftar di `DatabaseSeeder`, dan deploy menjalankan `db:seed --class=RmeBranchSeeder --force` eksplisit (presisi ENT-7 PermissionSeeder). (2) **Perawat memilih Cabang RME setelah login** memakai mekanisme Sprint 66 yang sama dengan Admin Klinik: `UserOnlineContext::ROLE_PERAWAT`, `requiresPerawatContext`/`isPerawatActive`/`startPerawatSession` (branch-only, `assertRmeBranch` menolak MAIN/non-RME/inaktif), route baru `POST rme/online-context/perawat` (`rme.online-context.perawat`, `StartPerawatOnlineContextRequest`), selector/badge/offline/login-redirect digeneralisasi; `resolveActiveBranchForAdmin()` kini mencakup Perawat sehingga registrasi kunjungan Perawat = perilaku Admin Klinik (branch dipaksa context, doctor auto-dari-ruangan). (3) **BranchContext priority baru**: active online context (Doctor/Admin Klinik/Perawat via `UserOnlineContextService::activeContextBranchId()`, fail closed: online + role match + cabang aktif RME; guarded `Schema::hasTable`) → `users.branch_id` → relasi `branches()` → MAIN/first-active. Konsekuensi: Lab Request/RME/pasien/antrian mengikuti cabang online terpilih; `users.branch_id` statis hanya fallback (rule 72 butir 14 tetap berlaku untuk role tanpa context). Branch selection TIDAK menambah permission. **Test-infra:** HTTP test role Perawat wajib `rmeMakePerawatActive($user, $branch)` (tests/Pest.php) atau 302 ke selector — 3 test existing diperbarui (Sidebar/PilotRoute/RoomAssignmentWorklist). Tests baru: `RmeBranchSeederTest` (6), `PerawatOnlineContextTest` (28, incl. lab request V2 SUN4 + isolasi + security). Regression green: RME+LabWorkflow 1142, ClinicVisit|Patient|Branch 997, Inventory+Ui+Navigation+Auth 1619. GO tag: `rme-branch-sun4-perawat-online-context-go`. Deploy note: tanpa migration; jalankan seeder SUN4 post-deploy (idempotent).

## FIX-LAB-TECHNICIAN-ROLE-ASSIGNMENT-UPLOAD-COMPRESSION (2026-07-11)

Branch `feature/fix-lab-technician-role-assignment-upload-compression` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`; do NOT target main). Baseline `rme-branch-sun4-perawat-online-context-go` @ `5e06954`. Doc: `docs/sprints/fix-lab-technician-role-assignment-upload-compression.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/74-lab-technician-assignment-upload-compression.mdc`. **Fix A — assignment teknisi:** target assignment (V2 `LabV2ProductionService::assignTechnician` + legacy `AssignmentService::assign/reassign` + dropdown `lab-v2-orders.show` & `production.show`) kini WAJIB user aktif ber-role Spatie canonical **`Technician`** via single source of truth `App\Modules\Technician\Services\TechnicianAssignmentEligibility` (master aktif + `user_id` ter-link + user tidak soft-deleted + `is_active` + `hasRole('Technician')`; `assertAssignable()` merevalidasi crafted `technician_id` server-side). **Permission `manage_production` ≠ role Technician** (override aktor, bukan kualifikasi target; Super Admin bisa meng-assign tapi tidak bisa DI-assign tanpa role). Pencabutan role hanya memblokir assignment BARU — histori tetap terbaca (board legacy tetap `listAll()` untuk filter historis); double-assign di-guard re-check dalam transaksi (state machine memegang row lock). Teknisi = aktor lab pusat lintas-cabang by design (tanpa batasan branch). Test-infra: `TechnicianFactory::assignable()` + helper `assignOrder()`/`technicianActor()`/`technicianWithUser()` menghasilkan target eligible. **Fix B — kompresi upload:** SEMUA evidence Lab Workflow V2 (7 tipe `LabWorkflowEvidence::TYPES` — SPK/model/pickup/handover/lokasi/2 signature) lewat pipeline canonical `LabWorkflowEvidenceService` → **`LabEvidenceImageOptimizer`** (GD, tanpa vendor baru) + `config/lab_workflow_uploads.php`: MIME dari bytes, bomb guard (50MP/12000px) sebelum decode, EXIF auto-orient lalu SEMUA EXIF/GPS terhapus via re-encode, polyglot dinetralkan, foto → **JPEG** (document 2200px q82→68 / photo 1800px q78→62 / delivery 1600px q75→62, hard max 1MB), signature tetap **PNG** transparan (canvas 1200×600, hard max 300KB), iterasi adaptif max 6 (quality dulu, lalu dimensi 0.85×) — masih melebihi hard cap → TOLAK (fail closed; tidak pernah di bawah min_quality), file kecil tidak diperbesar (`original_kept_smaller_v1`), output diverifikasi decodable. Fallback jujur runtime tanpa GD: simpan original tervalidasi ber-method `original_no_gd` (lokal dev CLI tanpa GD; CI + VPS ber-GD — CI extensions kini menyertakan `gd, exif`). Migration additive `2026_07_11_100001` menambah nullable `original_file_size`/`width`/`height`/`compression_method` pada `trx_lab_workflow_evidence` (bukti before/after; juga ke `sys_audit_logs`). Storage tetap privat + checksum + cleanup-on-failure; original besar tidak disimpan; evidence lama TIDAK di-recompress massal; PDF/SVG bukan tipe evidence (image-only by design; `AttachmentService` legacy tidak disentuh). Validasi: harness GD container PHP8.3 10/10 PASS (2021KB→478KB foto, 2423KB→513KB SPK, EXIF strip, alpha utuh); tests `LabTechnicianAssignmentEligibilityTest` (12) + `LabWorkflowUploadCompressionTest` (15, 8 GD-guarded jalan di CI). GO tag: `fix-lab-technician-role-assignment-upload-compression-go`. Deploy note: `php artisan migrate --force` (1 migration additive); verifikasi GD di VPS (`php -r "var_dump(extension_loaded('gd'));"`).

## FIX-ADMIN-LAB-LAB-ONLY-ACCESS — Restrict Admin Lab Role to Lab Module & Lab Workflow Only (2026-07-11)

Branch `feature/fix-admin-lab-lab-only-access` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`; do NOT target main). Baseline `fix-lab-technician-role-assignment-upload-compression-go` @ `32923d8`. Doc: `docs/sprints/fix-admin-lab-lab-only-access.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/75-admin-lab-lab-only-access.mdc`. **RBAC hardening — Admin Lab is now a LAB-ONLY role.** **No migration, no new/removed permission definition, seeder/config + landing redirect + audit command only; KTP/NIK never rendered; CICD-CTRL-1 gates preserved; Lab Workflow V2 canonical + legacy create still blocked.** Previously `Admin Lab` was massively over-privileged (held RME `manage_rme_billing`/`view+manage_clinic_visits`, Inventory+Procurement `manage_inventory`/`approve_inventory_purchase_request|order`/inventory analytics/executive/activity/cross-branch/`download_stock_transfer_checklist`, master data `manage patients|doctors|clinics|master data`+clinic master data, and `view dashboard`/`view_branch_dashboard`). **Root cause of the wider risk:** `Gate::before` (`RepositoryServiceProvider`) grants `Super Admin` a full bypass, so an operational "Lab Admin" account carrying `Super Admin` cannot be Lab-restricted by trimming the role — it must be demoted. **Changes:** (1) `RoleSeeder::ROLE_PERMISSIONS['Admin Lab']` rewritten to the Lab-only canonical set (Lab master data `manage lab services`/`manage technicians`; lab orders legacy+V2; `create_lab_branch_requests`/`manage_lab_pickups`; production/QC/delivery; legacy dental-lab billing `manage_invoice`/`view_invoice`/`create_invoice`/`issue_invoice`/`void_invoice`/`manage_payment`/`view_payment`/`create_payment` — Lab-domain, NOT RME cashier; legacy dental-lab reporting `manage_report`/`view_dashboard`/`view_*_report`/`export_report` — all Lab-scoped; `manage assignments`/`view reports`). New constant `RoleSeeder::ADMIN_LAB_REVOKED_NON_LAB` (19 non-Lab perms) is the single source of truth for the repair. (2) `AuthenticatedSessionController::redirectPathFor()` lands an `Admin Lab` (non-Super-Admin) user on `lab-v2-orders.index` (guarded `Route::has`) — it no longer holds `view dashboard` so the generic cross-module dashboard is 403. (3) New `App\Support\AccessControl\AdminLabLabOnlyAuditor` (read-only `audit()` + guarded `syncRole()`/`stripDirectRevokedFromAdminLabUsers()`/`demoteSuperAdminToAdminLab()` — refuses to leave zero Super Admins, only removes the Super Admin role, transactional `lockForUpdate`, privacy-safe) + thin command `php artisan rbac:admin-lab-lab-only-audit` (`--json`/`--strict` exit 2 on anomaly; `--sync-role`/`--strip-direct`/`--demote=<id>` explicit opt-in repairs). **Enforcement is 3-layer, not sidebar-only:** RME (`rme.*`), settings/master-data (`settings.*`), the dashboard route (`view dashboard|view_owner_dashboard`), and dev-console are gated by `permission:` route middleware → 403; the `inventory` route group has NO route-level permission middleware but every inventory controller calls `$this->authorize('viewAny', Model)` and the inventory policies key off `manage_inventory`/`view_inventory`/`manage master data` → Admin Lab (holding none) gets `AuthorizationException` → 403; the sidebar is purely permission-driven so removing the perms auto-hides RME/Inventory/Procurement/non-Lab master-data menus (sidebar is NOT the boundary). QC segregation stays enforced by the V2 state machine (Admin Lab keeping QC perms does not bypass producer-can't-QC-own); Technician eligibility + Lab evidence privacy/compression untouched. **Durable rules (mirror rule 75):** Admin Lab is Lab-only; never holds Super Admin; no RME/Inventory/Owner/Dev-Console/system access; sidebar ≠ security boundary; direct non-Lab route → 403; shared patient/doctor/branch labels ≠ CRUD grant; global report/export only if Lab-scoped; permission audit must use EFFECTIVE permissions (not direct-only `whereHas`); role changes seeder-only + `permission:cache-reset`; production role repair transactional+verified+guarded; primary Super Admin never modified. Tests: new `tests/Feature/AccessControl/AdminLabLabOnlyAccessTest.php` (16 — role matrix, effective perms/no Gate::before bypass, Lab routes 200, non-Lab routes 403, Lab-only sidebar, Lab landing redirect, auditor+command); updated 7 legacy over-privileged-contract tests (`RolePermissionHardeningTest`, `RoleManagementTest`, `InventoryPermissionHardeningTest`→denied, `PurchaseOrderPolicyTest`+`GoodsReceiptPolicyTest`→positive workflow moved to `Admin Warehouse`, `InventoryBranchComparisonAuthorizationTest`, `StockTransferChecklistPdfTest`) + `SidebarPermissionVisibilityTest` (Admin Lab sidebar direct + `/dashboard` 403) + `RmeLabCandidateE2EValidationTest` (cashier pages → Kasir actor). **Pre-existing (NOT this sprint):** `RmeSmokeTestRouteTest > Perawat` 302 (RME-BRANCH-SUN4 online-context selector; smoke seeder sets no Perawat context) — verified failing identically on base with changes stashed. GO tag on merge: `fix-admin-lab-lab-only-access-go`. Deploy: `db:seed --class=RoleSeeder --force` + `permission:cache-reset`; then `rbac:admin-lab-lab-only-audit` (verify) → `--strip-direct` → `--demote=<id>` only after verifying the Lab Admin account identity and that another Super Admin exists; never modify the primary Super Admin.

## HOTFIX-LAB-V2-NOTIFICATION-DESTINATION-ROUTING — Recipient-Aware Notification Destinations + Repair Broken Links (2026-07-11)

Branch `feature/hotfix-lab-v2-notification-destination-routing` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`; do NOT target main). Baseline `fix-admin-lab-lab-only-access-go` @ `2f5e39d`. Doc: `docs/sprints/hotfix-lab-v2-notification-destination-routing.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/76-lab-v2-notification-destination-routing.mdc`. Fixes the Lab Workflow V2 **"Model tiba di cabang"** notification 404 for Admin Lab. **No migration, no route, no permission, no role/seeder change; Admin Lab stays Lab-only; Workflow V2 canonical; legacy inactive for new orders; KTP/NIK never rendered; CICD-CTRL-1 gates preserved; in-app notifications only (no auto-WhatsApp).** **Root cause:** `LabDeliveryWorkflowService::completeDelivery()` built the DELIVERED notification URL **ad hoc** as `route('lab-workflow-requests.show', …)` and stored ONE URL for a **mixed audience** — `create_lab_branch_requests` is held by branch operators (Perawat/Admin Klinik) AND Admin Lab. `LabWorkflowRequestController@show` branch-isolates (`$order->branch_id !== BranchContext::requireId()` → 404); Admin Lab resolves to MAIN (not the order's branch) → **404**. Every other V2 notification already routed correctly (`lab-v2-orders.show` / `lab-pickup-tasks.show` / `lab-delivery-tasks.show`). **Fix:** (1) New canonical `App\Modules\LabOrder\Support\LabWorkflowNotificationDestinationResolver` — the single place a notification destination is computed, **per recipient**, returning an internal named-route URL the recipient can open or `null` (link-less); never widens a permission to open a link; branch page chosen only when the recipient's `BranchContext::forUser()` equals the order branch (else v2-order detail for lab staff, or the recipient's own queue index). 8 event keys (`EVENT_DELIVERED_TO_BRANCH` is the mixed one → branch operator gets `lab-workflow-requests.show`, Admin Lab/lab staff fall through to `lab-v2-orders.show`). (2) `LabWorkflowNotificationService` gains `notifyPermissionHoldersRouted()`/`notifyUsersRouted()` (resolve per recipient, one DB notification each); **all 9 lab notification call-sites across 6 services** (`LabPickupWorkflowService`, `LabWorkflowRequestService`, `LabDeliveryWorkflowService`×2, `LabV2QualityControlService`×2, `LabV2ProductionService`×2, `LabExternalDispatchService`) migrated off the now-`@deprecated` string-URL methods — no service hand-builds a URL. Safe because Technician/Quality Control/Courier/Delivery Coordinator all hold `view_lab_orders`, so every existing recipient keeps an identical openable link; only the DELIVERED-for-Admin-Lab case changes. (3) `NotificationController@read` open-redirect protection: the stored URL is followed only when same-origin internal (rejects external hosts, `//host`, `javascript:`/`data:`/`vbscript:`); URLs honor `config('app.url')`, never a hardcoded host. (4) Idempotent, dry-run-first `php artisan lab-workflow:repair-notification-destinations` (`--apply`/`--limit`/`--notification-id`/`--lab-order-id`/`--user-id`/`--json`/`--strict`) + `App\Modules\LabOrder\Services\LabWorkflowNotificationRepairService`: scans ONLY `LabWorkflowEvent` notifications, recomputes each recipient's destination via the same resolver, rewrites **only** `data.url` under a row lock, preserves id/read_at/created_at/notifiable/title/message/lab_order_id, fails closed on missing/soft-deleted order or missing recipient, skips legacy (non-V2) + unknown-event, idempotent (re-run = 0 pending), `--strict` exits 2 on pending/anomaly. Tests: new `LabWorkflowNotificationDestinationRoutingTest` (17 — resolver matrix, recipient-aware fan-out, Admin Lab opens v2 / 404s branch link + stays Lab-only, matched-branch operator opens branch page, open-redirect protection, ownership scoping) + `LabWorkflowRepairNotificationDestinationsTest` (10 — dry-run/apply/idempotent, scope safety, fail-closed, filters, command strict exit). Green: new 27; `tests/Feature/LabWorkflow` 157 passed / 8 GD-skipped; `AdminLab|Notification|SidebarPermissionVisibility|RolePermissionHardening` 70 passed. GO tag on merge: `hotfix-lab-v2-notification-destination-routing-go`. Deploy: **no migration/seed**; run `lab-workflow:repair-notification-destinations` (dry-run) → `--apply` → `--strict` (verify 0 pending) on VPS post-deploy.

## DEVFLOW-1 — Safe Sprint Acceleration, Reusable Foundations & Release Automation (2026-07-12)

Branch `feature/devflow-1-safe-sprint-acceleration-release-automation` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, baseline `hotfix-lab-v2-notification-destination-routing-go` @ `925a713`; do NOT target main). Doc: `docs/sprints/devflow-1-safe-sprint-acceleration-release-automation.md`; templates under `docs/engineering/`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/77-devflow-safe-sprint-acceleration.mdc`. **Foundation-track tooling sprint — reduces sprint/fix turnaround ~30–50% WITHOUT weakening security, branch isolation, ledger correctness, workflow state machines, required tests, release safety, backups, deploy evidence, smoke, or CICD-CTRL-1; no fake GO; no hidden skips.** **No migration, no permission change, no domain runtime change; all tooling is read-only + fail-closed.** Future prompts shrink to id/type/module/objective/acceptance-criteria — recurring rules are inherited from the canonical **Sprint Runtime Template** + a per-sprint **manifest**. Deliverables (all runnable + tested): (1) **Config source of truth** — `config/sprint_profiles.php` (10 sprint types HOTFIX/RUNTIME_FIX/MODULE_SPRINT/FOUNDATION_SPRINT/UI_POLISH/DATA_REPAIR/DOCS_ONLY/INFRA_RELEASE/SECURITY_FIX/MIGRATION_HEAVY → audit level 1/2/3 + CI profile + test/deploy/migration/rollback/evidence requirements), `config/sprint_regression_matrix.php` (changed paths → impacted category → focused Pest filters + related closure + CI escalation; fail-closed: unmatched file / unresolved diff / escalation category → full required suite), `config/shared_foundations.php` (canonical reusable services registry — BranchContext, effective-permission, notification destination resolver, LabEvidenceImageOptimizer, LabWorkflowEvidence, LabWorkflowStateMachine, TechnicianAssignmentEligibility, SensitiveValueMasker, AuditLogService, dompdf — reuse never duplicate), `config/devflow.php` (manifest schema + governance markers). (2) **Manifest** `.sprint/current.yml` + `.sprint/example.yml`; `App\Support\Devflow\SprintManifest` + `SprintManifestValidator` (schema + type + **manifest-vs-diff contradiction**: `schema_change=false` with a changed migration, `frontend_change=false` with changed JS/CSS/Vite, `security_impact=false` with a changed policy/middleware/permission → NO-GO; base `main`/`master` rejected). (3) **Services** `GitChangeInspector` (safe arg-array git, no shell interpolation), `SprintTestPlanner`, `SprintAuditPlanner`, `SprintScopeAuditor`, `SprintReleaseChecker`, `SprintEvidenceGenerator` (real values only; missing = NOT AVAILABLE; redacts secrets/13+-digit runs), `DevflowScanner`, `App\Services\Foundation\DevflowGovernanceService` (rules **DEVFLOW-R001..R010**). (4) **Commands** `sprint:new` / `sprint:prepare` (read-only preflight, mutates nothing) / `sprint:manifest-check` / `sprint:audit-plan` / `sprint:test-plan` / `sprint:test` (orchestrates Pest/Pint/build; never hides skips) / `sprint:scope-audit` / `sprint:release-check` (GO/WATCH/NO-GO, creates NO tag) / `sprint:evidence` / `foundation:devflow-check` / `foundation:shared-service-audit` (all `--json`/`--strict`, exit non-zero on NO-GO/FAIL). (5) **Release wrapper** `scripts/sprint-release.sh` — THIN orchestration over the existing `deploy-vps-runner.sh`/`rollback-vps.sh` (does NOT reimplement deploy/backup/smoke): `set -euo pipefail`, **dry-run default**, `--apply` gate, single-writer **release lock** (`storage/framework/devflow-release.lock`, stale-detect + `--force-lock` override), **GO tag only after a successful deploy + smoke and only with `--tag`**, no destructive DB reset / force-push (forbidden markers config-declared in `config/devflow.php`, scanned by `DevflowScanner` — the literals are intentionally kept out of the script's own comments to avoid the self-scan trap). (6) **Governance wiring** — informational `devflow_governance` section published into `architecture:foundation-governance-summary` via `FoundationGovernanceSummaryService` (NOT in the blocking `combinedDecision`; combined stays GO); optional evidence artifacts `devflow-check.json` + `shared-service-audit.json` added to `config/release_evidence.php` local/ci/vps `optional_artifacts` + `ReleaseEvidenceService` job map, captured by `scripts/ci/foundation-evidence-gates.sh` + `scripts/deploy-vps.sh` (additive, non-breaking — optional so a missing artifact never fails the NSF-10 gate). **Container bindings** for the base-path scanners in `AppServiceProvider` (`DevflowScanner`, `SharedFoundationScanner`, `GitChangeInspector`). Tests: `tests/Feature/Foundation/DevflowSprintToolingTest.php` (31 passed / 51 assertions). GO tag on merge: `devflow-1-safe-sprint-acceleration-release-automation-go`. Deploy note: **no migration/seed**; on VPS run `foundation:devflow-check --strict` + `foundation:shared-service-audit --strict` + `sprint:manifest-check --no-diff-check` post-deploy.

## LAB-WORKFLOW-V2-PILOT-UAT-1 — Pilot Operational Readiness, Real-Role UAT, SLA Baseline & Closure (2026-07-12)

Branch `feature/lab-workflow-v2-pilot-uat-1-operational-readiness-closure` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, baseline `devflow-1-safe-sprint-acceleration-release-automation-go` @ `51223539f940` verified exact; do NOT target main). Doc `docs/sprints/lab-workflow-v2-pilot-uat-1-operational-readiness-closure.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/78-lab-workflow-v2-pilot-uat-operational-readiness.mdc`; runbook `docs/operations/lab-workflow-v2-pilot-runbook.md` + real-role UAT checklist `docs/operations/lab-workflow-v2-pilot-uat-checklist.md`. Manifest `.sprint/current.yml` (MODULE_SPRINT, `sprint:manifest-check` GO). **Implementation-heavy operational-closure sprint. NO migration, NO permission/role change, NO runtime driver change; ledger untouched; KTP/NIK never rendered; Workflow V2 canonical + legacy inactive for new orders; all transitions via `LabWorkflowStateMachine`; Admin Lab Lab-only; QC segregation + delivery photo/signature gates + evidence privacy/compression + notification recipient-awareness all unchanged; no auto-WhatsApp.** DEVFLOW-1 used throughout (`sprint:prepare`/`manifest-check`/`audit-plan`/`test-plan`/`scope-audit`/`foundation:shared-service-audit --strict`/`foundation:devflow-check --strict` all GO). **Phase 2 — technician account closure (closes the documented "0 eligible technicians" blocker):** `App\Modules\Technician\Services\TechnicianAccountAuditor` (read-only `audit()` GO/WATCH/NO-GO — critical codes `no_eligible_technician`/`duplicate_user_link`; guarded `linkUser()` transactional + `lockForUpdate` + fail-closed + idempotent, **never changes the user's role**, refuses ambiguous links, projects eligibility in dry-run) + commands `lab:technician-account-audit {--json}{--strict}` (strict exits 2) and `lab:technician-link-user --technician= --user= [--dry-run|--apply] {--json}` (dry-run default, `--apply` to persist; refuses to link a user lacking the `Technician` role). **Phase 10 — pilot readiness gate:** `App\Modules\LabOrder\Services\LabWorkflowPilotReadinessAuditor` → one GO/WATCH/NO-GO aggregating V2-active/legacy-blocked/RME-branches/active-external-lab/eligible-technicians (reuses `TechnicianAssignmentEligibility`)/QC+courier+admin-lab actor availability/Admin-Lab-only (reuses `AdminLabLabOnlyAuditor`)/technician-account (reuses `TechnicianAccountAuditor`)/invalid-status (NO-GO)/stuck-orders (WATCH)/orphan tasks/failed jobs/evidence storage; every check guarded (errored → UNKNOWN, never a silent GO), free text masked via `SensitiveValueMasker`; only V2-inactive/no-RME-branch/no-eligible-technician/invalid-status emit NO-GO. Command `lab-workflow:pilot-readiness-audit {--json}{--strict}{--branch=}{--order=}`. **Phase 8 — operational dashboard:** `LabWorkflowOperationalDashboardService::overview()` V2-only status buckets via a single GROUP BY (no N+1), per-step production breakdown, delivered-today, overdue (idle>3d), recent-activity (order#+patient name+status+time only — no KTP/NIK/clinical). Scope server-side: `manage_lab_orders`→all branches; branch operator→own `BranchContext` branch **fail-closed to 0 if unresolved**; requested branch filter validated against `BranchService::rmeEnabledIds()` (IDOR-safe). Controller + `LabWorkflowDashboardRequest` + view `lab-workflow/dashboard/index.blade.php` + route `lab-workflow-dashboard.index` (`GET lab/operational-dashboard`, `permission:view_lab_orders|manage_lab_orders`) + sidebar "Dasbor Operasional Lab". **Phase 9 — SLA baseline:** `LabWorkflowSlaBaselineService::baseline()` per-stage durations from append-only `trx_lab_order_status_logs.changed_at` (never `updated_at`) — request→pickup, pickup→received, received→analysis, assignment wait, step 1–4, QC wait, external turnaround, model-done→delivery, delivery→delivered, total lead time; count/avg/median/min/max + rework count (QC_FAILED) + overdue; bounded (V2-only, date window, 2000-cap) + eager-loaded; labeled "Baseline pilot — bukan benchmark final". **Review-found cross-branch SLA leak closed:** the controller passes the dashboard's **fail-closed `effective_branch_id`** to the SLA service so an operator with an unresolved branch can never widen scope. Tests (29 new): `LabTechnicianAccountAuditTest` (12), `LabWorkflowPilotReadinessAuditTest` (7), `LabWorkflowSlaBaselineTest` (4), `LabWorkflowOperationalDashboardTest` (6). Workflow V2 canonical + legacy inactive; KTP/NIK never rendered/exported; immutable history; real-time aggregation (no summary table). **Migrations (additive only — `migrate`, never `migrate:fresh`/`db:wipe`):** `2026_07_12_100001..4` create `mst_lab_technician_capacity_profiles`, `mst_lab_service_workload_profiles`, `mst_lab_technician_capabilities`, `mst_lab_technician_availability_overrides` (all effective-dated, indexed; no profile ⇒ UNCONFIGURED/null, never fake 0); remaining workload = Σ items (service planned_workload × qty × band fraction; no workload profile ⇒ UNPLANNABLE); assigned_load (open orders with active `trx_lab_order_assignments`) vs unassigned_demand; utilization = assigned/available×100 (guarded, null on 0/missing); capacity_gap; backlog_coverage_days; deterministic due-risk pool simulation (overdue→due→priority→received→id) → ON_TRACK/AT_RISK/PROJECTED_LATE/OVERDUE/NO_DUE_DATE/UNPLANNABLE; explainable read-only recommendations (capability+capacity+active, ranked by projected utilization, reason codes when none — NEVER executes assignment). ## FIX-LOGIN-REDIRECT-RUNTIME-PERMISSIONS — Login Recovery & Deploy Runtime Permission Hardening (2026-07-12)

Branch `feature/fix-login-redirect-runtime-permissions` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, baseline `lab-prod-3-technician-capacity-planning-go` @ `7ad7c49`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/fix-login-redirect-runtime-permissions.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/82-login-redirect-runtime-permissions.mdc`; manifest `.sprint/current.yml` (RUNTIME_FIX). Fixes the VPS-pilot post-login **500** (Admin Lab userId 3) and **403**. **No migration, no permission/role/seeder change, no runtime driver change; Admin Lab stays Lab-only; ledger/RME untouched; KTP/NIK N/A.** **Root causes (verified):** (1) 500 = `scripts/deploy-vps.sh` ran all `php artisan` (gates, cache rebuild, `about`, smoke) as root with the lone `chown www-data` sandwiched *before* the final root-run artisan commands → PHP-FPM (www-data) could not write `storage/framework/cache/data` (Spatie `PermissionRegistrar` FileStore) / `storage/logs` → `file_put_contents … Permission denied` on the first post-login permission lookup. (2) 403 = `/dashboard` needs `permission:view dashboard|view_owner_dashboard`; Admin Lab holds neither. (3) `AuthenticatedSessionController::store()` used `redirect()->intended($default)` so a stored `url.intended=/dashboard` beat the role-aware default. (4) `release:automated-smoke` is guest-only (never touches the authorization + permission-cache path). **Fix:** (A) new `App\Services\Auth\PostAuthenticationRedirectService` = single source of truth for post-auth landing — role/permission-aware default (online-context select → Admin Warehouse exec dashboard → Admin Lab `lab-v2-orders.index` → generic dashboard) AND honors `url.intended` ONLY when internal + well-formed + route-resolvable + authorized (decided by the target route's own `permission:`/`role:` middleware vs the user's effective abilities, honoring `Gate::before`). (B) intended sanitizer drops external/`//host`/`javascript:`/`data:`/`vbscript:`/malformed/unknown-route/unauthorized → role-aware default; authorized internal intended (`/profile`, `/lab/v2-orders` for Admin Lab) preserved. (C) all 6 auth completion controllers (`AuthenticatedSession`/`RegisteredUser`/`VerifyEmail`/`EmailVerificationPrompt`/`EmailVerificationNotification`/`ConfirmablePassword`) route through the resolver — no more hardcoded `redirect()->intended(route('dashboard'))`. (D) `scripts/deploy-vps.sh` runtime-user hardening: detect PHP-FPM user (`RUNTIME_USER` → FPM pool `user =` → running worker → fallback `www-data`; fail closed on root), run cache commands as the runtime user via `runuser`, normalize ownership BEFORE the runtime rebuild AND again after ALL artisan/smoke commands (`chmod 2775` setgid dirs + `0664` files, never 0777), mandatory fail-closed writable gates (CACHE/SESSION/VIEW CACHE/LOG/BOOTSTRAP CACHE WRITE: GO → one FAIL aborts). (E) new `php artisan deploy:auth-landing-smoke --strict` (run as runtime user in deploy) proves credential-free + PII-free: Illuminate cache put/get, Spatie permission cache reset/load, role authorization matrix (Admin Lab default ≠ dashboard, Admin Lab may NOT reach `/dashboard` but MAY reach `/lab/v2-orders`, Super Admin MAY reach `/dashboard`); missing roles → WATCH (never fake GO); contradiction/cache-fail → NO_GO exit 1 → aborts deploy. **Guarantees:** no dashboard permission/Super Admin granted, `/dashboard` stays 403 for Admin Lab, Super Admin dashboard 200, no open redirect, session regeneration retained, backup-first + `migrate --force`-only, no `migrate:fresh`/`db:wipe`. Tests: `tests/Feature/Auth/PostAuthenticationRedirectTest.php` (13), `tests/Feature/Deploy/DeployRuntimePermissionsScriptTest.php` (9), `tests/Feature/Deploy/DeployAuthLandingSmokeCommandTest.php` (4). Green: new 26; Auth+AccessControl regression 95; DEVFLOW gates (manifest/scope/shared-service/devflow/security-compliance/ci-runtime-control/cicd-enterprise/roadmap next `MON-1` not stale) all GO; pint + `git diff --check` clean. GO tag on merge: `fix-login-redirect-runtime-permissions-go`. Deploy: **no migration/seed**; run `deploy-vps-runner.sh` ON the VPS; post-deploy authenticated smoke (Admin Lab `/lab/v2-orders` 200 not `/dashboard` 403; Super Admin dashboard 200).

## SATUSEHAT-1 — Readiness Foundation & Controlled Submission Filter (2026-07-16)

Branch `feature/satusehat-1-readiness-foundation-controlled-submission-filter` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, baseline `fix-login-redirect-runtime-permissions-go` @ `17fc2ad`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/satusehat-1-readiness-foundation-controlled-submission-filter.md`; governance `docs/architecture/satusehat-integration-readiness-governance.md`; runbook `docs/runbooks/satusehat-integration-readiness-runbook.md`; ADR `docs/adr/0001-satusehat-integration-readiness.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/83-satusehat-integration-readiness.mdc`; manifest `.sprint/current.yml` (MODULE_SPRINT). **Readiness foundation only — DaengtisiaMS is prepared to integrate with SATUSEHAT later but SATUSEHAT-1 makes NO external network call (no OAuth, no IHS lookup, no FHIR POST).** New bounded context `App\Modules\Satusehat`. **Migrations additive only** (`migrate`, never `migrate:fresh`/`db:wipe`): `mst_satusehat_code_mappings`, `mst_satusehat_entity_identifiers`, `trx_satusehat_candidates`, `trx_satusehat_submission_batches`, `trx_satusehat_submission_items`, `trx_satusehat_audit_logs` (append-only, no updated_at). **Gateway:** `SatusehatGatewayInterface` bound (fail-closed) to `DisabledSatusehatGateway` (default, opens no socket; `SATUSEHAT_ENABLED=false`), `FakeSatusehatGateway` (tests), `HttpSatusehatGateway` (SATUSEHAT-2 placeholder, never runs). `config/satusehat.php` (all SATUSEHAT_* environment variables default safe/blank) + governance flags `satusehat.integration_readiness`/`satusehat.external_submission_enabled` in `config/feature_flags.php` (both default OFF, risk critical). **Aturan permanen:** (1) no auto-send from any event (RME finalize/odontogram/visit completion/payment/scheduler/observer/webhook/login/dashboard/preview) — events only create/refresh a candidate idempotently post-commit (`SatusehatVisitObserver`/`SatusehatMedicalRecordObserver` via `DB::afterCommit`; failure logged, never rolls back clinical/billing tx). (2) Every completed visit + FINAL medical record → **candidate only**; explicit review/filter required; no "Send All"/"select all across pages". (3) Approve/exclude server-side + branch-scoped to RME-enabled branches (IDOR-safe via `SatusehatCandidateRepository::idsInBranches`/policy `inRmeBranch`); exclude requires a reason. (4) **Deterministic source hash** (`SatusehatSourceHasher`, no random/drift-only fields, NIK hashed not stored) → clinical drift after approval ⇒ `source_changed` + approval revoked (`approved_by` retained, `revoked_at` set) + re-review. (5) Readiness engine (`SatusehatReadinessService`, 16 gates → `ready|incomplete|blocked|source_changed`, PII-free reason codes). (6) Local FHIR preview (`SatusehatFhirPreviewBuilder`, Encounter/Condition/Procedure, WITA→UTC reinterpret, masked NIK, distinguishes local reference vs future remote IHS id, NO odontogram/scan/handwriting, honest label "belum dikirim dan belum diverifikasi oleh API SATUSEHAT"; Condition never fabricated — no structured diagnosis source). (7) Queue/prepare is **fail-closed** (`SatusehatSubmissionService`: prepares a local batch, audits `queue_attempted`+`queue_blocked`, sends nothing). (8) Mappings versioned (one ACTIVE per key, never edited in place — new draft version; PG partial-unique + service enforcement for SQLite); identifiers single-active per (environment, entity, local id), entered manually (no external lookup); sandbox ≠ production. (9) NIK always masked (`maskKtp`); sensitive eligibility/preview snapshots `encrypted:array` (first encrypted casts in the codebase); `trx_satusehat_audit_logs` append-only + PII-free via `SensitiveValueMasker`. **RBAC (5 new permissions):** `view_satusehat_submissions` (Owner, Supervisor RME), `review_satusehat_submissions` (Owner, Supervisor RME), `send_satusehat_submissions` (Supervisor RME), `manage_satusehat_mappings` (Supervisor RME), `manage_satusehat_settings` (Supervisor RME); Super Admin via `Gate::before`; Doctor/Kasir/Perawat none. **Routes** `satusehat.{submissions,mappings,identifiers}.*` under `/rme/satusehat` (standalone `auth` group, permission-gated); sidebar "SATUSEHAT" group (independently gated). Command `satusehat:backfill-candidates` (`--dry-run`/`--branch`/`--from`/`--to`/`--limit`/`--json`; bounded, idempotent, no network). Provider `App\Modules\Satusehat\Providers\SatusehatServiceProvider` (bindings + fail-closed gateway + policies + observers) registered in `bootstrap/providers.php`. **Architecture:** Controller (thin, `AuthorizesRequests`) → FormRequest → Service → RepositoryInterface → Repository → Model; branch from `BranchContext`/`BranchService::rmeEnabledIds()`, never the request. Tests: `tests/Feature/Satusehat/` (Foundation/Candidate/Observer/SubmissionFilter/MappingIdentifier/Backfill) — 35 passed. Regression: RME critical (`MedicalRecordFinalization|RmeDoctorCashierCompletionGate|RmeRoomAssignmentGate|CashierBilling|RmePayment|PatientOutstandingReceivableCarryOver|PatientCentricRmWorkspace`) 147 passed; broad (`ClinicVisit|MedicalRecord|Odontogram|RolePermissionHardening|SidebarPermissionVisibility|PilotRouteAuthorization|FeatureFlag|LabIntegration|RoleManagement`) 428 passed; pint + `git diff --check` clean; `npm run build` pass. GO tag on merge: `satusehat-1-readiness-foundation-controlled-submission-filter-go`. Deploy note: `php artisan migrate --force` (6 additive tables) + `db:seed --class=PermissionSeeder --force` + `db:seed --class=RoleSeeder --force` + `permission:cache-reset`; `SATUSEHAT_ENABLED` stays `false`. **Out of scope (SATUSEHAT-2):** OAuth2 runtime, token refresh, real IHS lookup, sandbox/production API requests, Encounter/Condition/Procedure POST, KFA/medication, live reconciliation, odontogram mapping, DocumentReference upload, production cutover. **Next sprint: SATUSEHAT-2 — Sandbox API Adapter & FHIR Submission.**

Authoritative CI run `29472089584` = SUCCESS on the exact candidate SHA `0492d4e` (CICD-CTRL Classifier / NSF-R012 Quality / NSF-R011 Critical / CICD-CTRL Selective Module / NSF-9 Release Safety+Smoke / NSF-10 Release Evidence all pass; NSF-R011 Full-Suite skipped per standing pattern). Independent security review before merge (no material findings on network/injection/transaction/audit/PII-leak; 2 minor findings FIXED in `0492d4e`: `refresh()` write-behind-read-gate → new `SatusehatCandidatePolicy::refresh` requires `review_satusehat_submissions`; candidate listing query no longer hydrates `ktp_number`). Annotated GO tag `satusehat-1-readiness-foundation-controlled-submission-filter-go` (tag object `85c79df`) @ `315e289` — local/origin/VPS exact-match. **VPS pilot deployed** inside the VPS (`srv1730088`, `/var/www/asia-dental-lab-v2`) via `bash scripts/deploy-vps-runner.sh start` (detached, SSH-safe; runner exit=0, DEPLOY OK): pre-deploy backup `pre_auto_deploy_20260716-051854.sql` (NSF-10 backup-verify GO); 6 additive SATUSEHAT migrations Ran; env=pilot, debug OFF, maintenance OFF; post-deploy `db:seed --class=PermissionSeeder|RoleSeeder --force` + `permission:cache-reset` (as www-data) registered 5/5 SATUSEHAT permissions. **No-network verified on pilot:** `config('satusehat.enabled')=false`, gateway=`DisabledSatusehatGateway`, env=sandbox. HTTP smoke (bare IP `145.79.13.224`): `/login` 200 (title "Daengtisia Management System"), `/dashboard` 302, `/rme/satusehat/submissions`+`/rme/satusehat/mappings` 302 (guest auth redirect, no 500), `/health/live`+`/health/ready` 200; `release:automated-smoke` GO; 0 new Laravel errors. **Deploy gotcha (pre-existing infra, NOT SATUSEHAT):** at deploy time the DaengtisiaMS pilot's HTTP entry point was shadowed on the shared VPS by a co-tenant app **aish-pos** (nginx symlinks `aish-pos`/`aishpos.online` added 2026-07-12) — both apps were catch-all on port 80 with NO `default_server`, so aish-pos (loaded first) answered the bare IP and every DaengtisiaMS route returned nginx's default 404 (a non-SATUSEHAT route `/login` 404'd identically). Fixed (owner-authorized) by adding `listen 80 default_server;` to `/etc/nginx/sites-available/asia-dental-lab` (backup `asia-dental-lab.bak-20260716-satusehat1`; `nginx -t` OK; reload) — DaengtisiaMS is the bare-IP app (its `APP_URL`), `aishpos.online` keeps its domain via exact `server_name` match (verified still 301, not broken). **Lesson: this shared VPS now hosts two apps on port 80; DaengtisiaMS must retain `default_server` or a dedicated domain, else a future co-tenant nginx change can shadow the pilot's HTTP entry again.**

Controller `batchIndex/batchShow/queue` + routes `satusehat.submissions.batches.{index,show,queue}` + `satusehat.identifiers.verify`; views `satusehat/submissions/{batches,batch-show}.blade.php` (sandbox badge, kill-switch status, per-batch send). **Aturan permanen (mirror rule 84):** production disabled + fail-closed; sandbox host allowlist enforced before every call; TLS never disabled; secret/token never logged/stored/returned; token only via client_credentials cached+locked; no auto-send / no "Send All" / explicit review+queue by `send_satusehat_submissions`; queue() fail-closed while disabled; source hash re-verified (drift aborts); succeeded resource never resent; ambiguous outcome → reconcile not re-POST; retry classified+bounded, 429 honors Retry-After; NIK/odontogram/scan/handwriting never sent; terminology never invented; branch isolation server-side; no HTTP inside a DB tx; clinical/billing tx never rolled back by SATUSEHAT; live verify uses synthetic data; production activation is a separate sprint. **Feature flag** `satusehat.external_submission_enabled` → `implemented` (adapter exists, OFF by default). Security review: no CRITICAL/HIGH (secret/token/TLS/SSRF/duplicate/PII/IDOR/tx/fail-closed all clean); 2 LOW — a latent NIK-in-URL in the unused legacy `verifyIdentifier` path (hardened with a NIK-pattern guard) + OAuth token in the single-VPS `file` cache (standard Laravel, keep private). fixed ⇒ auto-resolve `revalidated`; regressed/expired-waiver ⇒ reopen same row). `SatusehatRemediationService` lifecycle (acknowledge/assign/start/request-review/resolve/waive/reopen — locked, audited via new append-only `SatusehatAuditLog` EVENT_ISSUE_*): **resolve = revalidation only** (still-failing ⇒ ValidationException), **hard issues can never be waived**, waivers never change the canonical readiness verdict. **Workspace** routes `satusehat.readiness.*` under `/rme/satusehat/readiness` (read = `view_satusehat_readiness|manage_satusehat_remediation`; writes = `manage_satusehat_remediation`; waive = `manage_satusehat_readiness_waivers`): dashboard (KPI + candidate board with ONE operational status per row via `SatusehatOperationalStatusResolver`, practitioner/org/location readiness — IHS NEVER fabricated, statuses `ready_for_lookup|awaiting_external_identifier|identifier_present_unverified|verified_*`, treatment-mapping summary, onboarding checklist `SatusehatOnboardingChecklistService` — external items honestly `blocked_external`), issues list/detail/audit-timeline. Resolver precedence: SOURCE_CHANGED → LOCAL_CONFORMANCE_FAILED/UNSUPPORTED_LOCAL_SCHEMA → INVALID_PATIENT_DEMOGRAPHICS → internal gaps (patient/practitioner/location/diagnosis/mapping/dental) → AWAITING_* → **BLOCKED_EXTERNAL_CREDENTIAL** (only external IHS gaps remain) → READY_INTERNAL; odontogram-less visits are dental-informational (no dental Observation emitted), never blocking. **Structured diagnosis:** doctor entry card on the RM page (Alpine autocomplete `rme.diagnoses.search`, ACTIVE-only — synthetic/deprecated excluded) via `rme.visits.medical-record.diagnoses.store|destroy` (MedicalRecordPolicy::update + room gate); master governance `satusehat.diagnoses.*` (`manage_structured_diagnoses`) + `ClinicalDiagnosisSeeder` (15 WHO ICD-10 dental codes, master ≠ mapping); readiness G10 upgraded — no diagnosis ⇒ info `diagnosis_not_structured` (facts key `diagnoses` OMITTED when empty so legacy candidate hashes stay byte-stable), unmapped ⇒ `diagnosis_mapping_missing` incomplete (mapping lookup `findActive(env,'diagnosis',master_id,code,Condition)` — mapping rows need BOTH local_entity_id + local_code); post-approval diagnosis change drifts hash ⇒ approval revoked. Legacy RM never backfilled/auto-coded. **Synthetic pack** `satusehat:synthetic-pilot seed|verify|reset` (`--confirm` for writes; no factories — VPS is `--no-dev`): isolated branch `SYN4A` + marker `[SYNTHETIC-SATUSEHAT-4A]`, synthetic KTP `9999999999994401`, synthetic-only mappings keyed to synthetic entity ids (never shared codes → zero contamination), visit needs `created_by` (NOT NULL) resolved from actor/first user; reset deletes ONLY campaign records + deactivates/soft-deletes the branch. **Rehearsal** `satusehat:rehearse --synthetic [--dry-run|--prepare-batch --confirm]`: 9 stages (pack→candidate→readiness→issues→deterministic re-hash→local FHIR preview→operational classification→outbound gate OFF=pass→production guard blocked=pass); final state honestly `BLOCKED_EXTERNAL_CREDENTIAL` (never submitted/succeeded). **Diagnostics:** `satusehat:diagnose` (posture booleans only — never a credential value), `satusehat:readiness-audit` (GO/WATCH; `--strict` fails on open HARD/drift), `satusehat:data-quality-scan` (dry-run default, bounded by `scan.max_batch_size`), `satusehat:queue-health`, `satusehat:reconciliation-status`; SATUSEHAT-3 `satusehat:production-guard-check` unchanged. **RBAC (4 new permissions only — no orphan permissions):** `view_satusehat_readiness` (Owner, Supervisor RME, Admin Klinik), `manage_satusehat_remediation` (Supervisor RME, Admin Klinik), `manage_satusehat_readiness_waivers` + `manage_structured_diagnoses` (Supervisor RME only); Doctor/Kasir/Admin Lab none; `SatusehatDataQualityIssuePolicy` adds the RME-branch IDOR boundary (empty branch set = fail-closed deny). **Test-infra gotchas:** `expectsOutputToContain` consumes ONE writeln per expectation — a single multiline JSON `$this->line()` satisfies only the first (use `Artisan::call`+`Artisan::output()`); satusehat workspace HTTP tests with role users need `withoutMiddleware(EnsureRmeOnlineContext::class)` + `seedAccessControl()`. Full SATUSEHAT dir 162 passed (SATUSEHAT-1/2/3 invariants intact). ## LEGACY-RME-PDF-1A — Legacy RME PDF Archive Foundation: Schema, Permission & Date Rules (2026-08-06)

Branch `feature/legacy-rme-pdf-1a-schema-permission-date-rules` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, baseline SATUSEHAT-4D merge `3cd6452`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/legacy-rme-pdf-1a-schema-permission-date-rules.md`; ADR `docs/adr/0008-legacy-rme-pdf-archive-foundation.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/90-legacy-rme-pdf-archive.mdc`; manifest `.sprint/current.yml`. **Foundation for "Import Arsip RME Lama Pasien" — the PDF a Super Admin will later upload is the patient's HISTORICAL RME data, not a template and not a live-workflow RME.** Operated from **Master Data → Master Data RME → Import Arsip RME Lama**. **Scope 1A = schema, permissions, policies, repositories, date-rule domain, audit foundation, config, tests ONLY.** Deliberately NOT shipped: PDF conversion/Poppler/Imagick/OCR/canvas/PDF.js, upload runtime, annotation, thumbnails, publish runtime, patient-timeline integration, doctor viewer, legacy print/PDF — and **no route, controller or Blade view exists in 1A** (an endpoint was not added merely for coverage; services are tested directly). Feature flag `rme.legacy_pdf_archive` (`FEATURE_RME_LEGACY_PDF_ARCHIVE`, risk `high`) **defaults OFF**, resolved via `LegacyRmeFeatureGuard`→`FeatureFlagService` (never `config('feature_flags…')` — dots break traversal). New bounded context `App\Modules\LegacyRme` (Interfaces/Models/Policies/Repositories/Services/Support; no module provider — bindings + policies go in `RepositoryServiceProvider`'s `private array $repositories`/`$policies`).

**Migrations (additive only — `migrate`, never `migrate:fresh`/`db:wipe`):** `2026_08_06_100001..4` create `stg_rme_legacy_imports`, `stg_rme_legacy_import_pages`, `trx_rme_legacy_records`, `trx_rme_legacy_record_pages`. Legacy records are deliberately NOT stored in `trx_medical_records` (which requires a NOT NULL + UNIQUE `clinic_visit_id` and drives the visit→cashier workflow) — a legacy RME has **no `clinic_visit_id` and never creates a fake visit**. Constraints: `UNIQUE(uuid)`, `UNIQUE(legacy_import_id,page_number)`, `UNIQUE(rme_legacy_record_id,page_number)`, **`UNIQUE(source_import_id)`** (one staging batch → at most one record, publishing idempotent), indexes on `(patient_id,rme_date)`, `(patient_id,status)`, `source_pdf_sha256`, `normalized_content_hash`. `source_pdf_sha256` is intentionally **not globally unique** (cross-patient duplicate investigation + VOID-then-reimport need repeats). Staging soft-deletes; **published tables carry NO soft delete** — immutability is the contract. FK posture: patient/branch RESTRICT/NULL (never cascade clinical rows), staging pages cascade with their batch, published pages RESTRICT (evidence).

**Domain contract (permanent, mirrored in rule 90):** (1) legacy RME = archive of a patient's OLD RME data from a historical PDF; (2) never creates a clinic visit, invoice, payment, consent, odontogram, `LabCaseCandidate`, `LabOrder` or SATUSEHAT candidate, never changes visit status, never enters visit/revenue KPI; (3) the date is **chosen manually** from what the document shows — never upload time / `created_at` / file date / PDF metadata / OCR — stored as `selected_rme_date` (staging) / `rme_date` (published), separate from `uploaded_at`/`published_at`; (4) **`legacy date < earliest_native_rme_date` STRICTLY** (equal = rejected, since equal is exactly the overlap case) **when a native RME exists — widened by FIX-ROLL2-1 to every date the document represents (`latest < earliest native RME`)**, plus `legacy date < today` (**today is rejected** — an archive is historical) and `patient birth date <= legacy date` (**equal to the birth date is accepted**); `mst_patients.date_of_birth` is nullable so a missing value **skips** that rule and is never invented; (5) **SUPERSEDED by LEGACY-RME-PDF-FIX-ROLL2-1** — 1A refused a patient with no native RME (`PATIENT_HAS_NO_NATIVE_RME`); such a patient is now ELIGIBLE (the native RME is a bound, not a prerequisite) and a native encounter is never manufactured to unlock an import; (6) an existing legacy row (staged/published/**VOID**) is never native RME and never becomes the comparison point; (7) a published record is **immutable** — correction is **VOID with a reason + fresh import**, never an in-place edit or hard delete.

**Canonical clinical date:** `earliest_native_rme_date` derives from **`trx_clinic_visits.visit_date`** via the visit that owns the medical record. `trx_medical_records` has **no clinical date column at all**. Rejected (each a real trap): `created_at` (Sprint 59 removed the finalize lock + Sprint 64.0 lets a doctor open a sheet from a later visit → row written long after the encounter), `finalized_at` (nullable workflow timestamp), `canonical_visit_id` (a cache — its own migration says the resolver is the live source of truth), `mst_patients.registered_at` (administrative). **Single source of truth = `App\Modules\LegacyRme\Services\PatientEarliestNativeRmeDateResolver`** backed by new `ClinicVisitRepository::earliestVisitWithMedicalRecordForPatient(int $patientId)` (+ interface) — no controller/FormRequest/policy/view/test may re-derive it. Excludes cancelled visits, visits without an MR, soft-deleted visits/records, and every legacy row; orders `(visit_date, id)` for PG/SQLite stability. **Deliberately NOT branch-scoped** (diverges from `PatientRmWorkspaceResolver::resolveCanonicalVisit()` on purpose, pinned by a test): a narrower scan could only move the bound LATER and admit an overlapping document — access control is a separate concern. **Timezone:** "today" uses `legacy_rme.dates.clinical_timezone` (defaults to the app timezone — the same wall clock `ClinicVisitService` uses when stamping `visit_date` via `Carbon::today()`), and `visit_date` is treated as an opaque calendar date and never shifted; realigning the setting moves the boundary by at most one calendar day.

**Rules service:** `LegacyRmeDateRuleService` with stable codes (`PATIENT_HAS_NO_NATIVE_RME` **removed by FIX-ROLL2-1**; `LEGACY_DATE_RANGE_INVALID` added) `LEGACY_DATE_NOT_BEFORE_NATIVE_RME`, `LEGACY_DATE_IN_FUTURE` (covers today + future), `LEGACY_DATE_BEFORE_PATIENT_BIRTH`, `LEGACY_DATE_INVALID`; `evaluate()` returns an immutable `LegacyRmeDateRuleResult` (branch on the CODE, never the message), `assert()` throws `ValidationException`, `snapshotCutoff()` produces the server-side `earliest_native_rme_date_snapshot` — **the cutoff is always recomputed server-side; a client-supplied snapshot is never trusted**. The rule context is PII-free by construction (dates + `patient_id` only).

**Status domain:** no native PHP `enum` exists anywhere in `app/`, so vocabularies are final Support classes + model constants — `LegacyRmeImportStatus` (`DRAFT→UPLOADED→QUEUED→PROCESSING→READY_FOR_REVIEW→REVIEWED→PUBLISHED` + `FAILED`/`CANCELLED`, explicit `TRANSITIONS`, terminal = PUBLISHED/CANCELLED), `LegacyRmeImportPageStatus` (fail-closed: default `PENDING`, only `READY` is publishable), `LegacyRmeRecordStatus` (`PUBLISHED→VOID` only).

**RBAC (5 new permissions, granted to NO operational role):** `view_legacy_rme_imports`, `create_legacy_rme_imports`, `review_legacy_rme_imports`, `publish_legacy_rme_imports`, `void_legacy_rme_imports` — Super Admin (the designated Master Data RME operator) holds them via the existing RoleSeeder `'*'` grant + the single global `Gate::before`; classified into the existing `rme` `PermissionGroupingService` group. Policies `LegacyRmeImportPolicy` (viewAny/view/create/review/publish/cancel) + `LegacyRmeRecordPolicy` (viewAny/view/void; **`update`/`delete` hard-wired `false`**) registered in `RepositoryServiceProvider::$policies` (`private`, not `protected` — copying stock Laravel registers nothing; there is no `AuthServiceProvider`). No policy `before()` hook (it would shadow the single global bypass). Branch isolation via `LegacyRmeWorkspaceScope` (governance tier = all RME-enabled branches, others pinned to `BranchContext::forUser`, **empty scope = deny-all**); **`origin_branch_id` is provenance metadata, never authorization**, and the branch is never read from the request. Repositories take the resolved branch-id list as the first parameter and fail closed (`whereRaw('1 = 0')`) on an empty scope.

**Audit:** reuses the shared `sys_audit_logs` trail through `AuditLogService` (no new audit table). `LegacyRmeAuditService` adds the payload policy the shared service lacks — every payload is filtered against `LegacyRmeAuditEvent::ALLOWED_METADATA_KEYS` and reduced to length-bounded scalars. Events `LEGACY_RME_IMPORT_CREATED`/`DATE_SELECTED`/`PUBLISH_REJECTED`/`DUPLICATE_DETECTED`/`PUBLISHED`/`VOIDED`. Never persisted: patient name, KTP/NIK, clinical content, base64, raw PDF, absolute path.

**Test-infra gotcha:** module models must declare `protected static function newFactory()` — Laravel otherwise resolves `Database\Factories\Modules\LegacyRme\Models\…Factory` and fails (all factories live in `database/factories/`, `Database\Factories\`). Tests: `tests/Feature/LegacyRme/{LegacyRmeSchemaTest,LegacyRmeFoundationTest,PatientEarliestNativeRmeDateResolverTest,LegacyRmeDateRuleServiceTest,LegacyRmePermissionTest,LegacyRmeNonRegressionTest}` (73). The suite is wired into the **CI critical filter** (`.github/workflows/foundation-evidence-gates.yml` — appended `|LegacyRme|PatientEarliestNativeRmeDateResolver`) because the CICD-CTRL-1 Selective Module Gate branches only on Inventory/Lab/Ui/Permission and `run_rme_tests` is consumed by no job. Deploy: `php artisan migrate --force` (4 additive tables) + `db:seed --class=PermissionSeeder --force` + `permission:cache-reset`; keep `FEATURE_RME_LEGACY_PDF_ARCHIVE` false. **Next: LEGACY-RME-PDF-1B** — upload runtime, page rendering, review UI, publish workflow, private-file serving, timeline integration and the doctor viewer, all behind the same flag.

## LEGACY-RME-PDF-1B — Private Upload Runtime, PDF Validation, Queue Processing & Page Rendering (2026-08-06)

Branch `feature/legacy-rme-pdf-1b-private-upload-rendering` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, baseline LEGACY-RME-PDF-1A merge `0b37ce4` / GO tag `legacy-rme-pdf-1a-schema-permission-date-rules-go`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/legacy-rme-pdf-1b-private-upload-rendering.md`; ADR amendment in `docs/adr/0008-legacy-rme-pdf-archive-foundation.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/90-legacy-rme-pdf-archive.mdc` (rules 21–35). Implements the intake runtime: **Super Admin → Master Data RME → Impor Arsip RME Lama → pick patient (Nomor RM) → choose the legacy date MANUALLY → upload PDF → validate → private storage → QUEUE → `pdfinfo` inspect → `pdftoppm` rasterize + thumbnails → page rows → `READY_FOR_REVIEW`.** **NO migration** (1A already shipped every column). **No new permission** — the five 1A permissions are the boundary. `FEATURE_RME_LEGACY_PDF_ARCHIVE` stays **false**.

**Deliberately NOT in 1B:** review approval, publish, promotion into `trx_rme_legacy_records`, patient timeline, doctor viewer, legacy print, annotation/pen/eraser/canvas, OCR, automatic date extraction, VOID runtime. **There is no publish route** (asserted by test). `READY_FOR_REVIEW` is the furthest a document goes.

**Private storage:** new disk `legacy_rme_private` (`driver local`, root `storage_path('app/legacy-rme-private')`, **`serve => false`**, `visibility private`, `throw true`). Rooted **outside** the `local` disk root on purpose: Laravel registers a `storage.{disk}` route for a local disk with `serve => true`, so anything under `storage/app/private` is in principle addressable by a signed framework URL — clinical pages must not be. `LegacyRmeStorageService::assertPrivateDisk()` refuses a disk that is forbidden/public/carries a `url`/has `serve` enabled. Paths are opaque: `rme-legacy/imports/p{patient_id}/{import_uuid}/{source,pages,thumbnails,processing}` — never a name/KTP/phone/diagnosis, and the uploaded filename is a sanitized display label only, never part of a path.

**PDF pipeline:** contracts `LegacyRmePdfInspectorInterface` → `PopplerLegacyRmePdfInspector`, `LegacyRmePdfRasterizerInterface` → `PopplerLegacyRmePdfRasterizer`, `LegacyRmeMalwareScannerInterface` → `ConfiguredLegacyRmeMalwareScanner` (OFF by default; when disabled it reports "not scanned", **never** "passed"), plus `Fake*` doubles so no test needs Poppler. `LegacyRmeProcessRunner` is the only place a binary runs — always an **argument array** through Symfony `Process` (execve, no shell), explicit timeout, binary from config; stderr/exit code go to the log only, never onto the exception (whose message is rendered AND persisted to `failure_message`). **Thumbnails use Poppler `-scale-to`, not GD/Imagick** — the local dev machine has no GD and an image dep would make rendering environment-dependent; they are PNG, and `getimagesize()` (core, no extension) validates pages. **`pdftoppm` zero-pads its suffix to the page-count width** (9 pages → `-1`, 10 → `-01`), so output is always discovered by scanning the dir and parsing the suffix — never by assuming a filename (real-Poppler test covers the 12-page case). Stable codes in `LegacyRmePdfFailure` (22 of them); callers branch on the **code**, never the message.

**Upload order:** feature flag → 1A date rules (never re-derived) → origin-branch validation → structural validation + server-side SHA-256 → duplicate precheck → malware scan → store private PDF → `DB::transaction` create staging row → **after commit** dispatch. The file is written before the transaction (a filesystem write cannot join one); a failed transaction deletes the orphan (tested). **The malware scan runs at upload, not in the job** — infected bytes are then never stored at all, and re-scanning immutable bytes on retry adds no security.

**Duplicate policy (server-computed SHA-256; filename irrelevant both ways):** PUBLISHED record → block (same or other patient); **VOID record → allow** (1A defines correction as "VOID plus a fresh import", so blocking would break the documented path — the collision is audited); active staging → block; FAILED same-patient → block and point at **Retry** (no second staging row); FAILED/CANCELLED **other** patient → allow (the mis-assignment fix must stay possible).

**Queue:** `ProcessLegacyRmePdfImport` extends ENT-5 `EnterpriseQueueJob`, `ShouldBeUnique`, queue `legacy-rme-documents`, carries **only the import id**. Status claim is one locked transaction (duplicate delivery = no-op); stale output + stale page rows cleared before rendering; pages upsert on `UNIQUE(legacy_import_id,page_number)`; temp dir removed in `finally`. **Queue retries do not drive domain failure** — a failure lands `FAILED` with a stable code and stops, and the operator's explicit Retry (`FAILED → QUEUED`, legal in the 1A map) is the recovery path; `failed()` covers a worker that died before recording anything. **No `READY_FOR_REVIEW → QUEUED` edge was invented** (nor `FAILED` abused to fake one) — a rendered document is corrected by cancelling and re-uploading. A stale `PROCESSING` (worker died) becomes reclaimable after 2× timeout + margin.

**HTTP:** 9 routes `settings.rme.legacy-imports.{index,create,store,show,status,source,pages.show,retry,cancel}` under `/settings/rme/legacy-imports`. **Deliberately NOT nested inside the `manage patients` group** — 1A defines the five legacy permissions as *the* boundary, and inheriting a second requirement would make them insufficient alone (a view-only holder opening the index is asserted). Three independent gates per action: route `permission:` middleware, controller feature-flag check (**disabled ⇒ 404**, so the surface reveals nothing), and the policy (per-row branch scope; new abilities `retry` + `viewFile`). `{import}` is a plain integer resolved through `findByIdInBranches()` with the caller's scope — **out-of-scope is 404, not 403**, so ids cannot be probed; a page is looked up **through** its import so a page number cannot cross imports. File responses: explicit Content-Type, `nosniff`, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, generic download name (path and original filename never echoed).

**Security-review outcome:** two candidate findings were raised and both filtered to confidence 3 (below the ≥8 bar) — an equivalent, broader patient enumeration already ships via `settings/patients/{patient}/edit` (which even renders full KTP, for more roles), and the `origin_branch_id` issue is unreachable while only Super Admin holds the permission. **Both cheap fixes were applied anyway** because 1C will delegate these permissions: (1) `resolveOriginBranch()` now validates against the **uploader's own scope** and anchors a scoped operator's branchless upload to their own branch — the 1A claim that `origin_branch_id` is "provenance, never authorization" was **factually wrong** (repository listings filter on it, the policy scopes on it), and rule 14 has been corrected; (2) `assertPrivateDisk()` also refuses a disk with `serve` enabled; (3) patient selection by surrogate key goes through `LegacyRmePatientLookupService::findSelectable()`, applying the same doctor patient scope as the canonical Nomor-RM search, on **both** `create()` and `store()`.

**Test-infra:** new Pest helpers in `tests/Pest.php` — `legacyRmePdfBytes()` builds a structurally valid multi-page PDF in pure PHP (correct xref; no fixture binary committed, no clinical document used), `legacyRmePdfUpload()`, `legacyRmeNativeVisit()`, and `legacyRmeArchiveFlag()` (**the flag key contains a dot, so it is a literal array key — dot-notation config cannot reach it; the whole `feature_flags.flags` array must be rewritten**). Role-user HTTP tests need `withoutMiddleware(EnsureRmeOnlineContext::class)` (class lives in `App\Modules\RmeOnlineContext\Middleware`). `BranchContext` falls back to the first active RME-enabled branch, so branch-scope tests must pin `users.branch_id` explicitly or they pass for the wrong reason. **The 1A assertion "registers no HTTP route for the legacy RME archive" was replaced, not left to pass by accident** — its substring filters (`legacy-rme`/`legacy_rme`/`rme-legacy`) would not have matched the new `legacy-imports` naming, so it would have kept "passing" while the surface existed; it now asserts the exact staging route set **and** that `publish`/`review`/`approve`/`void` still have no endpoint.

**Config:** `config/legacy_rme.php` gains a `processing` block keeping the 1A `LEGACY_RME_*` env family (not a second `LEGACY_RME_PDF_*` family): `LEGACY_RME_DISK` (now `legacy_rme_private`), `_DPI` 180, `_THUMBNAIL_MAX_EDGE` 320, `_PROCESS_TIMEOUT` 180, `_MAX_PAGE_PIXELS` 40M, `_MAX_PAGE_DIMENSION_PT` 20000, `_MAX_RENDER_BYTES` 200MiB, `_QUEUE` `legacy-rme-documents`, `_TRIES` 3, `_MALWARE_SCAN` false. Tests: 7 suites, **179 passed / 551 assertions**; native RME regression 158 passed; permission/sidebar 42 passed. **Runtime dependency: `poppler-utils` (`pdfinfo`, `pdftoppm`)** on any host that processes documents; the real-Poppler suite **skips** (never silently passes) when absent. The `legacy-rme-documents` queue must be served by the managed worker before the flag is enabled. Deploy: **no migration, no seeder**. GO tag on merge: `legacy-rme-pdf-1b-private-upload-rendering-go`. **Next: LEGACY-RME-PDF-1C** — review approval, publish into `trx_rme_legacy_records`, patient-timeline integration, doctor viewer, legacy print.

## CICD-CTRL-3 — Dedicated Self-Hosted CI Runner (2026-08-07)

Branch `feature/cicd-ctrl-3-dedicated-self-hosted-ci-runner` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report` @ `0b37ce4`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/cicd-ctrl-3-dedicated-self-hosted-ci-runner.md`; runbook `docs/runbooks/self-hosted-runner.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/91-dedicated-self-hosted-ci-runner.mdc`; manifest `.sprint/current.yml` (INFRA_RELEASE). **Status: `CLOSED / GO`.** PR #267 merged as `9484dd9`; closure PR #268 merged as `cbe9712`; annotated GO tag `cicd-ctrl-3-dedicated-self-hosted-ci-runner-go` (tag object `4afb3de`) targets **`cbe9712`** (the CTRL-3D closure merge, *not* `9484dd9`). **No migration, no permission, no product runtime change, no deploy.** The sprint closed carrying a documented residual of **9 pre-existing Full Suite failures** — that residual was closed by CICD-FIX-6 (`fe36f06`) and formally retired by **CICD-BASELINE-REVERIFY-1**; the current expected Full Suite failure baseline is **0**.
