## FIX-LEGACY-RME-ROUTINE-OPS-1 — Routine Batch Operational Surface, Time-Bounded Governance & SOD Staffing Truth (2026-08-19)

Branch `feature/fix-legacy-rme-routine-ops-1` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report` @ `b8d7053`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/fix-legacy-rme-routine-ops-1.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/106-legacy-rme-routine-batch-operations.mdc`; manifest `.sprint/current.yml` (RUNTIME_FIX, `sprint:manifest-check` GO). **No migration, no permission, no role/seeder, no policy, no route, no runtime driver change; `scripts/deploy-vps.sh` untouched.** Legacy RME stays a historical archive — no native ClinicVisit/MedicalRecord/Invoice/Payment/Odontogram/Prescription/LabOrder/SATUSEHAT side effects; published legacy history stays readable with the migration flag OFF; KTP/NIK never rendered.

**Incident.** `ROUTINE-20260819-TKM1-01` (TKM1, quota 25) registered over SSH with `planned_start_date=null` / `planned_end_date=null`; `legacy-rme:ops-readiness` returned WATCH `batch_binding` + WATCH `batch_window` ("approval has no expiry") although the routine batch runbook always required a window. Batch cancelled canonically, ledger clean (accepted/published/in_flight/failed/unexplained/quota_drift all 0). **`ROUTINE-20260819-TKM1-01` is immutable audit evidence — never deleted, never reused.**

**Root cause A (CLI, CONFIRMED).** `LegacyRmeWaveGovernanceService::createWave()` already accepted `?string $plannedStartDate/$plannedEndDate`, the model/columns/HTTP controller already carried them — but `LegacyRmeWaveAdminCommand::register()` passed only 6 named args and the `$signature` had no date options (the sole "planned" option, `--planned-documents`, is a count used by `branchQuota()`). **Root cause B (UI, CONFIRMED).** `StoreLegacyRmeMigrationWaveRequest` validated the dates and the controller forwarded them, but `settings/rme/migration-operations/index.blade.php` never rendered the inputs. The two official surfaces had *different* gaps, which is why the invariant survived.

**Root cause C (SOD readiness, CONFIRMED).** `checkSeparationOfDuties()` read two config booleans and nothing else — no user/role/account query — so an enforced-but-unstaffable control reported GO.

**Root cause C′ (briefed RBAC drift) — DOES NOT REPRODUCE. No RBAC repair was required or performed.** Measured live: user 11 `Supervisor RME` has `approve_legacy_rme_migration_wave=YES`, `manage=NO`, `create=NO`, `publish=YES` — exactly canonical; `RoleSeeder` already grants it; `PERMISSIONS_NOT_REGISTERED=[]`. The briefed "approve = NO" was a **measurement artifact reproducible two ways**: (1) user 11 has **0 direct permission rows**, so a direct-only `whereHas('permissions')` audit reports a false NO; (2) `LegacyRmeMigrationWavePolicy::approve()` is `can(...) && ! $wave->isTerminal()`, and with `ROUTINE-...-01` CANCELLED and **0 non-terminal waves in production**, a policy probe returns false for *every* account. Proof: effective `can()`=YES, policy vs terminal wave=false, policy vs an in-memory non-terminal wave=true. **`RoleSeeder` was NOT run as a repair — there was no delta**, and `syncPermissions` REMOVES role permissions absent from the array, so running it needlessly is a real risk.

**Root cause C″ (deploy reconciliation).** `scripts/deploy-vps.sh` contains **no seeding at all**; every prior sprint's RBAC reconciliation was a manual post-deploy step. Adding `RoleSeeder` to every deploy was **deliberately rejected** (destructive `syncPermissions` unattended; the script is scanned by ENT-10/11/12 governance gates; the missing capability was *detection*, not application). The durable control is the readiness check, which now fails closed on this class of gap.

**Implementation.** New `App\Modules\LegacyRme\Support\LegacyRmeBatchWindowRule` — the shared, caller-agnostic window invariant (strict `Y-m-d`, calendar-real dates via a `createFromFormat` round trip so `2026-02-31` is refused rather than rolled into March, inclusive end, `end >= start`, presence required by policy, parsed in the clinical timezone). **Asserted inside `createWave()`**, the single node Graphify shows both callers reach directly — not in the FormRequest, whose own docblock already records that a request-layer rule is one "where a second caller (the CLI) would bypass it entirely". It deliberately does **not** compare against today (a batch may be registered ahead of its start; a lapsed window is the `batch_window` readiness finding, not a reason to refuse the write). New `App\Modules\LegacyRme\Support\LegacyRmeSodStaffing` — capability via effective `can()` (honours direct + role grants + the single global `Gate::before` Super Admin bypass), candidates narrowed to permission holders ∪ Super Admins so the query stays bounded, inactive/soft-deleted excluded, **counts and booleans only (never a name/email/id)**; a lone all-powerful account is **not** a separated pair; deliberately **not** routed through the wave policy (its terminal-wave clause would report "nobody can approve" whenever the newest batch is COMPLETED/CANCELLED). **The document chain is file → review → publish, not file → publish** — `SeparatePublisherGuard::GUARDED_ACTIONS` is `[REVIEW, PUBLISH]` and `LegacyRmeImportStatus::TRANSITIONS` only allows `READY_FOR_REVIEW → REVIEWED → PUBLISHED`, so an import nobody else can review can never be published by anyone; `document_chain_staffed` requires a distinct maker/reviewer pair AND a distinct maker/publisher pair (a create-vs-publish-only model reported a false GO and was caught in review). `checkSeparationOfDuties()` now emits `FAIL` + new stop-the-line INCIDENT code `SOD_STAFFING_UNAVAILABLE` when an enforced rule cannot be staffed, while **preserving precedence** (publisher-disabled still reports as publisher-disabled; approver-off is still the documented WATCH) and **preserving the honest `human_separation_verifiable_by_application => false` disclaimer**, now paired with `account_separation_verifiable_by_application => true`. CLI gains `--planned-start-date` / `--planned-end-date` (dry run echoes the intended window + `batch_window_required` and still writes nothing). Form gains **Tanggal Mulai Batch** / **Tanggal Berakhir Batch** (`type="date"`, `old()` preserved, inline errors). Config: `legacy_rme_operations.routine_batch_window.required` (default **true**), `legacy_rme_operations.sod_staffing.max_accounts_scanned`, `SOD_STAFFING_UNAVAILABLE` in `legacy_rme_steady_state.stop_the_line` + `severity.incident_codes`.

**Durable rules (mirror `.cursor/rules/106`).** (1) A routine batch is time-bounded; every new batch declares a bounded planned window, end inclusive, `end >= start`, clinical calendar (Asia/Makassar), never server UTC. (2) One rule for CLI and HTTP — **the CLI is never a weaker entry point**; when a wave field is added, add the CLI option in the same change. (3) Missing window **fails closed** at registration; nothing is written. (4) **Historical waves are immutable** — never backfilled to satisfy a rule invented afterwards. (5) `WAVE-3` stays SKIPPED/NOT REQUIRED and retired; cancelled batch codes are never reused. (6) SOD switches state *intent*; **distinct accounts able to perform each half are required** (creator≠approver, maker≠publisher) and an enforced-but-unstaffable control is a stop-the-line FAIL, worse than one deliberately off. (7) Account separation is verifiable, **human separation is not** — report both, never conflate. (8) **Always audit EFFECTIVE permissions (`can()`)**, never direct-only; **never decide capability from a policy probe against an arbitrary wave** — both traps produced the same false negative here. (9) Supervisor RME = checker/approver (never `manage_*`/`create_*`); Admin Klinik = branch intake maker (never publisher). (10) Role repair goes through canonical `RoleSeeder` (idempotent `syncPermissions`, inspect the delta first), never ad-hoc `givePermissionTo()` and never SQL. (11) At rest: capability OFF, admission EMPTY, no active batch, `AT_REST`. (12) A routine batch needs no engineering GO tag; a software/governance change like this one does.

**Review-found defects fixed before merge (4 adversarial lenses, 6 real defects — two of them inside the staffing check itself):** (a) HIGH — staffing omitted `review_legacy_rme_imports`, reporting GO for a chain that dead-ends at review (found independently by two lenses); (a2) MEDIUM/HIGH — **the chain must be satisfiable by ONE maker**: ANDing `distinct_maker_reviewer` with `distinct_maker_publisher` lets a DIFFERENT maker satisfy each, so `A=create+publish` + `B=create+review` reported GO while nothing was publishable (A files → publish needs A the uploader; B files → review needs B the uploader). `chainStaffed()` now requires a single maker with a reviewer other than them AND a publisher other than them; reviewer and publisher MAY be the same account (the seeded Supervisor RME checker legitimately holds both — `SeparatePublisherGuard` only excludes the uploader); (b) `parse()` resolved the clinical timezone INSIDE its `catch (Throwable)`, so a misconfigured `CLINICAL_TIMEZONE` surfaced as "your date is malformed" instead of the fail-loud `InvalidClinicalTimezoneException` — resolve the timezone OUTSIDE the try; (c) `0000-01-01` survives the strict round trip and PostgreSQL then rejects it at INSERT as an unhandled `QueryException` (500 instead of a field error) — year `< 1` now refused; (d) `(bool) env(...)` is the unsafe pattern this codebase already documents — a present-but-empty `LEGACY_RME_ROUTINE_BATCH_WINDOW_REQUIRED=` casts to false and silently disables the invariant, so it reuses the existing fail-safe `SeparatePublisherGuard::resolveEnabledFromEnv()`; (e) the Blade form hardcoded `required`, so `required=false` was honoured by service+CLI but not the browser — now passed from the controller.

**Tests.** New `tests/Feature/LegacyRme/LegacyRmeRoutineBatchWindowTest.php` (29) + `LegacyRmeSodStaffingReadinessTest.php` (16). Updated: `LegacyRmeWaveSeparationOfDutiesTest` + `LegacyRmeMigrationReconciliationTest` wave fixtures declare a window; `LegacyRmeSteadyStateOpsTest` `beforeEach` staffs SOD; new `tests/Pest.php` helper `legacyRmeStaffSeparationOfDuties()` (governance manager + branch maker + separate checker). **`SupervisorRmeRolePermissionTest` needed no repin — `RoleSeeder` is unchanged.** Existing `batch_window` readiness coverage left as-is.

**Test-infra gotcha (diagnosed, not guessed).** Whole-directory `tests/Feature/LegacyRme` runs showed "file not found in storage" failures — `SOURCE_FILE_MISSING`, "Berkas sumber/gambar halaman tidak ditemukan pada penyimpanan" — hitting a **different test every run**. Root cause is **two concurrent `php artisan test` processes in the SAME worktree** (here, a review subagent running the suite while the main run was in flight): `Storage::fake('legacy_rme_private')` DELETES `storage/framework/testing/disks/...` at fake time, so whichever process fakes first destroys the other's files. It is not order-dependence (no randomisation is configured), not a codebase flake, and not caused by a wave/governance change — the same file passes 25/25 in isolation and a base-control worktree running alone is clean. **Before diagnosing any LegacyRme storage failure, run `ps -eo pid,cmd | grep pestphp/pest/bin/pest` and confirm the suite is running alone in that worktree.** Generalises the earlier `pkill`-orphan note: ANY concurrent test process in the same worktree causes it, orphaned or not.

**FIX-LEGACY-RME-ROUTINE-OPS-1 SHIPPED + VPS DEPLOYED (2026-08-19) — WATCH, NO GO TAG:** PR #315 squash-merged as `acbf5e3835dc082df14f9a1784331c929ba593de`. Pre-merge CI run `32202230947` SUCCESS on the exact candidate `826dacc` (NSF-R012 Quality / CICD-CTRL Classifier / CICD-CTRL Selective Module / NSF-R011 Critical / NSF-9 / NSF-10 all pass; the sibling Critical variant is skipped by design). **FULL SUITE: SKIPPED BY EXPLICIT USER DECISION — EXECUTION COUNT 0.** Audited across all four sprint runs: every Full Suite job records `startedAt == completedAt` (zero duration) and never executed — three were collateral of superseded PR runs, one (`32205563992`, the post-merge base push) was cancelled immediately because a base-branch push is one of the three triggers that fires the gate automatically. No dispatch was made and **no CI file was modified to suppress it**; on `pull_request` the gate skips through the workflow's own condition. Consequently **NO GO tag was created or moved** — `fix-legacy-rme-routine-ops-1-go` stays reserved for a follow-up closure sprint that runs one authoritative Full Suite (expected failures 0) and makes the final GO decision. Targeted regression, run **in isolation**: **1010 passed / 5 skipped / 0 failed** (3128 assertions) across `tests/Feature/LegacyRme` + `AccessControl` + `Auth`; the 5 skips are all GD-guarded (dompdf image decode; CI installs `gd, exif, poppler-utils`). All 11 mandated suites ran and passed incl. `LegacyRmeWaveSeparationOfDuties`, `LegacyRmeWaveApprovalUiReachability`, `LegacyRmeSteadyStateOps`, `LegacyRmeMigrationOperationsGate`, `LegacyRmeImportAuthorization`, `LegacyRmeMigrationReconciliation`, `LegacyRmeImportLifecycleCli`, `SupervisorRmeRolePermission`, `LegacyRmeProgramClosureContract`. pint + `git diff --check` clean; DEVFLOW manifest/scope/devflow/shared-service/ci-runtime-control/security-compliance all GO. **VPS deployed** via `bash scripts/deploy-vps-runner.sh start` run ON `srv1730088`: `exit=0`, `DEPLOY OK: 20260819-013808`, `DEPLOY_HEAD_TARGET_MATCH=YES`, `immutable-exec rc=0`, `DEPLOY_SNAPSHOT_CLEANED=YES`, deploy governance gates 10/10 GO; VPS HEAD == merge SHA exactly. **Production smoke over `https://daengtisia.online`** (never the bare IP): `/login` `/health/live` `/health/ready` `/health/lb` 200, guest `/dashboard` + `/rme/satusehat/submissions` 302; env pilot, debug OFF, maintenance OFF, `queue:failed` empty, `laravel.log` untouched since 2026-08-18 23:08 → **0 new errors from this deploy**. **Deploy-smoke gotcha (expected, not a fault):** the bundled `SMOKE-HTTP-HEALTH` probes `http://127.0.0.1/login` and returns **404** on this shared VPS because the co-tenant owns the localhost/bare-IP path — always verify externally over the domain; an inside-VPS probe is not evidence either way. **Production RBAC verified, NOT repaired** (user 1 Super Admin/Owner all-Y; user 7 Admin Klinik create-only; user 11 Supervisor RME approve+review+publish, manage/create N) — canonical already, so no seeder was run. **New logic live on production:** `document_chain_staffed=true` (creator 1/approver 2/maker 2/reviewer 2/publisher 2, both distinct pairs), `routine_batch_window.required=true` with absent/reversed/impossible windows all REFUSED and a valid single-day window ACCEPTED, CLI `--planned-start-date`/`--planned-end-date` present; `legacy-rme:ops-readiness` → GO / AT_REST / `Ready for a routine batch: YES` with `separation_of_duties` now reading "…**and each is staffed by distinct accounts**"; `legacy-rme:rollout-readiness --expect=off` → GO. **Immutability confirmed:** 0 non-terminal waves, `ROUTINE-20260819-TKM1-01` still present/CANCELLED/`start=null end=null`, 4 historical null-window waves unchanged — nothing backfilled, nothing deleted. Closing posture: **IMPLEMENTED / TARGETED TESTS PASS / REQUIRED NON-FULL-SUITE CI PASS / MERGED / DEPLOYED / PRODUCTION SMOKE PASS / RBAC RECONCILED (verified no-op) / OPS READINESS PASS / RULES SYNCHRONIZED / FULL SUITE SKIPPED BY EXPLICIT USER DECISION / WATCH — PENDING FINAL FULL SUITE CLOSURE.** No routine TKM1 batch was opened; that remains a separate operator action requiring its own approval and a bounded window, under a NEW code (never `ROUTINE-20260819-TKM1-01`).

## GLOBAL TEMPORARY FULL-SUITE POLICY — **ACTIVE** (2026-08-19)

**Canonical:** `docs/governance/global-temporary-full-suite-policy.md` · **rule mirror:** `.cursor/rules/107-global-temporary-full-suite-policy.mdc` (`alwaysApply: true`). Status **ACTIVE / TEMPORARY / user-authorised / global across the current corrective-fix series**. Governance + docs + rules only — **no runtime, config, CI, schema, permission or deploy change** (this change set is deliberately `docs_only`, verified with `scripts/ci/resolve-gates.sh`, so enabling the policy cannot itself fire the gate it governs).

**The rule.** While ACTIVE, an **individual fix sprint MUST NOT run the Full Suite**. It is **deferred** — never removed — to **one authoritative consolidated run on the frozen final integrated SHA** once every planned fix is complete. Rationale: repeating an *integrated* gate after each fix re-validates a SHA the next fix immediately invalidates. Never write "the Full Suite is unnecessary"; the correct statement is "the Full Suite is deferred."

**Nothing else is relaxed.** Every fix still requires targeted tests, **dependency-aware cumulative regression** (derived from `sprint:test-plan` + `graphify`, never the whole repo and never too little), security + architecture review, `pint`, `git diff --check`, permission/module suites, **all required non-Full-Suite CI gates** (NSF-R012 Quality, CICD-CTRL Classifier, NSF-R011 Critical, CICD-CTRL Selective Module, NSF-9 Release Safety & Smoke, NSF-10 Release Evidence), merge + deploy verification, production smoke, rules sync, cleanup. *"Full Suite skipped, therefore other tests may be skipped"* is **forbidden reasoning**.

**Biggest accidental-trigger risk is LOCAL, not CI.** `php artisan sprint:test --all-required` (and `--regression`) appends a `pest-full-required` step that runs **bare `php artisan test` — the whole repository** whenever `escalate_full_suite` is true, and escalation is **fail-closed** (a changed file matching an escalation category *or no category at all* sets it; `.sprint/current.yml` matches nothing). The canonical sprint template otherwise tells every sprint to run exactly that before release — **while this policy is ACTIVE that instruction is superseded**. Use `sprint:test-plan` (read-only) + `sprint:test --focused` (never escalates) + `vendor/bin/pint --dirty --test` + `git diff --check` + `php artisan test --filter='<targeted regression>'`.

**CI mechanics (verified, not assumed; superseded in part by CI-TEMP-FULL-SUITE-SCHEDULE-GATE — see that section below, where BOTH automatic triggers are now gated in CI).** Before that gate, `full_suite_gate` fired on exactly three events: `pull_request` → **never** (so a fix sprint's own PR is structurally `FULL_SUITE_EXECUTION_COUNT=0`); **`push` to base → fires** (a squash-merge *is* such a push); `schedule` (weekly `0 2 * * 0` UTC) → fires, calendar-driven and attributable to no sprint; `workflow_dispatch` + `run_full_suite=true` → deliberate and **forbidden while ACTIVE**. **Gotcha:** `.sprint/current.yml` matches no classifier pattern and falls through to `unknown_high_risk`, so because every real sprint updates its manifest, **every real fix merge enables the post-merge Full Suite step** — only a genuinely docs-only change set resolves to `docs_only` and skips it. Always resolve the profile with `scripts/ci/resolve-gates.sh` before pushing; never guess.

**Post-merge run handling (precedent: FIX-LEGACY-RME-ROUTINE-OPS-1, run `32205563992`).** An official `gh run cancel` is permitted **only** when the pre-merge candidate was green on an **identical tree** (cancelling also cancels the other gates in that run), the cancel lands **before the Full Suite job begins executing tests**, the run ID + `startedAt == completedAt` zero-duration proof are recorded, and **no CI file is touched**. **Forbidden:** cancelling a Full Suite that has already begun executing in order to report a zero count — that is an execution; record it and report the partial result. Never cancel a *scheduled* run for count management; a scheduled run is an informational baseline and can never be cited as a fix sprint's Full Suite pass.

**Evidence — read the STEP, never the job.** Verified on real runs: on a **docs-only push to base the `NSF-R011 Full Suite Gate` job reports `success` while the suite never executed** (classifier resolves `docs_only` → `Run full Pest suite` = `skipped`, and a `Note skipped full suite (docs-only change)` step writes the evidence; nothing failed, so the job is green). Run `32211741144` on `1311dba` is the worked example. So the rule is not merely "job skipped ≠ passed" — **`success` ≠ tests passed**; a green Full Suite job proves only that nothing *errored*. Verify with `gh run view <id> --json jobs --jq '.jobs[]|select(.name=="NSF-R011 Full Suite Gate")|.steps[]|select(.name|test("full Pest suite"))|"\(.conclusion)\t\(.name)"'`; the run-level `conclusion=success` from `gh run list` is an aggregate and says nothing about execution.

**Evidence.** Every report states `FULL_SUITE_STATUS = SKIPPED_BY_GLOBAL_TEMPORARY_POLICY`, `FULL_SUITE_EXECUTION_COUNT = 0`, `GLOBAL_TEMPORARY_FULL_SUITE_POLICY_ACTIVE = YES`. **Job skipped ≠ tests passed. Job cancelled ≠ tests passed.** Never claim a pass that did not run; if an accidental Full Suite genuinely runs, record run ID/SHA/event/result, report the breach, and never rewrite history to protect a count. Historical GO tags and CI runs are **immutable** — this policy is prospective only.

**Closure + GO tags.** A finished fix closes at **`WATCH — PENDING CONSOLIDATED FULL SUITE`** — a valid closure, not a failure; distinguish *implementation closure* (reached) from *final integrated verification closure* (deferred). **No final engineering GO tag** for a fix whose governance requires a Full Suite before GO. The consolidated closure (policy §9) starts **only** on explicit user authorisation: inventory WATCH sprints → verify merged+deployed → freeze the final SHA → baseline re-verify → **one** Full Suite (expected failures **0**) → production smoke → governance audit → GO decision → tags. On failure: **NO FINAL GO**, record the exact failure set, **do not immediately rerun**, diagnose with targeted tests, fix, merge/deploy, then agree the next authoritative run with the user.

**Unchanged:** deployment (`bash scripts/deploy-vps-runner.sh start` **on the VPS**, real `exit=0` + `DEPLOY OK`, backup first, `migrate --force` only, never `migrate:fresh`/`db:wipe`), production smoke over `https://daengtisia.online`, Legacy RME fail-closed / RM-derived branch authority / read-only archive / maker ≠ publisher / creator ≠ approver / admission EMPTY + capability OFF at rest, CICD-CTRL-1 default-strong classification, branch isolation, ledger-only inventory, RBAC 3-layer, PII masking, additive-only migrations. **This policy changes test scheduling only.**

**Retirement:** ends only on explicit user revocation **or** completed consolidated closure — one finished fix does not end it. On retirement, mark the doc and rule `RETIRED after consolidated Full Suite closure`; **never delete them.**

**Deferred inventory:** `FIX-LEGACY-RME-ROUTINE-OPS-1` — WATCH, Full Suite skipped (count 0), **no GO tag**; runtime authority `acbf5e3`, docs-only `1311dba` undeployed. Per explicit user decision **do not create a `FIX-LEGACY-RME-ROUTINE-OPS-1A` closure sprint now** — it folds into the single consolidated closure. Append a row to policy §12 for each further fix shipped under this policy.

## CI-TEMP-FULL-SUITE-SCHEDULE-GATE — Temporary Scheduled Full-Suite Suppression (2026-08-19)

Branch `feature/ci-temp-full-suite-schedule-gate` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, verified base authority `917e4c3`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/ci-temp-full-suite-schedule-gate.md`; canonical policy `docs/governance/global-temporary-full-suite-policy.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/107-global-temporary-full-suite-policy.mdc`. **CI/governance sprint, not an application feature sprint. No migration, no permission, no route, no schema, no clinical/ledger runtime change.**

**Root cause.** PRs #317/#318 established the GLOBAL TEMPORARY FULL-SUITE POLICY as **prose only** — deliberately, so enabling it could not fire the gate it governs. Nothing in CI enforced it, so **two** automatic paths still reached the Full Suite: the weekly `schedule` (`0 2 * * 0` UTC) **and — the larger, easily-missed gap — `push` to the base branch, i.e. every fix squash-merge**, which policy §6.1 mitigated only after the fact via a manual `gh run cancel` precedent. Policy **§6.2 explicitly reserved** narrowing these triggers as an option "requiring separate explicit user authorisation"; this sprint **is** that authorisation, so the change is pre-sanctioned rather than unilateral.

**What changed.** (1) New canonical machine-readable state `.github/ci-policy/full-suite-policy.json` — the status lives **once**; `config/ci_runtime_control.php` declares only the *contract* (allowed statuses, deferred events, required reason codes, resolver markers) and a test asserts it carries no duplicate `status`/`active` key. (2) New fail-closed, read-only, dependency-free bash resolver `scripts/ci/resolve-full-suite-policy.sh` → `temporary_full_suite_policy_active` / `full_suite_authorized` / `full_suite_defer_reason` / `policy_status` / `policy_source`. (3) Workflow: the `classify` job (always-on, GitHub-hosted) resolves and publishes the decision as job outputs, so **a skipped Full Suite still records WHY**; `full_suite_gate` now additionally requires `needs.classify.outputs.full_suite_authorized == 'true'`; new `workflow_dispatch` input `full_suite_policy_override` (default false). (4) `CiRuntimeControlScanner::temporaryFullSuitePolicyPosture()` + governance rules **CICDCTRL-R012/R013/R014**, wired into `foundation:ci-runtime-control-check` (now 6 checks, GO).

**Durable rules.** While the policy is ACTIVE: `schedule` and `push`-to-base are **DEFERRED** (`TEMPORARY_FULL_SUITE_POLICY_ACTIVE`); a dispatch with `run_full_suite=true` but no override is **DEFERRED** (`..._OVERRIDE_REQUIRED`); **both** inputs together are the authorised consolidated-closure path (`AUTHORISED_CONSOLIDATED_FULL_SUITE`), which a test proves stays reachable. Resolution **fails closed** — missing/unreadable/unknown/duplicated status ⇒ `POLICY_STATE_UNRESOLVED_FAIL_CLOSED` ⇒ not authorised (failing closed can only defer a run, never hide a failure). The gate is **deferred, never deleted**: both triggers and the job remain, `full_suite_fallback_triggers` still holds, the three always-on gates (quality / NSF-9 / NSF-10) are untouched, and `paths-ignore` stays absent. **Defence in depth:** the original event conditions are RETAINED and ANDed with the authorisation check, so even a resolver bug could only ever narrow, never widen. Policy §7.1 is preserved: **a green Full Suite job still does not mean the suite ran — read the step.** Restoring the automatic cadence requires flipping the canonical status to `RETIRED` — a deliberate governance act reserved for the consolidated closure, **never** a side effect of another sprint.

**Gotcha.** Symfony's YAML parser keeps the workflow's `on:` key as the **string** `'on'` (PyYAML coerces it to boolean `true`) — a Pest test reading `$workflow[true]` fails with "Undefined array key 1". Use `$workflow['on']`.

Tests: `tests/Feature/Cicd/TemporaryFullSuiteScheduleGateTest.php` (24 / 149 assertions) — the real resolver's output is fed into an evaluator of the **real workflow `if:` expression**, so the gate is proven unable to fire *without ever firing it*. Green: Cicd regression 118 passed / 589 assertions; `foundation:ci-runtime-control-check --strict` / `cicd-enterprise-gate-check` / `security-compliance-check` / `enterprise-documentation-check` all GO; `sprint:manifest-check` GO; pint + `git diff --check` clean.

**Shipped + VPS deployed (2026-08-19).** PR #319 squash-merged as `cf6d2cc`; candidate CI run `32230944053` all required gates green (Classifier/Quality/Critical/Selective/NSF-9/NSF-10; Full Suite **skipped**). Annotated GO tag `ci-temp-full-suite-schedule-gate-go` (tag object `75aca46`) @ `cf6d2cc` — local/remote/VPS exact-match. Deployed via `scripts/deploy-vps-runner.sh start` **on the VPS**: `exit=0`, `DEPLOY OK: 20260819-104010`, `Nothing to migrate.`; production smoke over `https://daengtisia.online` — `/login` + `/health/live` + `/health/ready` + `/health/lb` 200, all components ok, gated routes 302, **0 Laravel errors**, no failed jobs; Legacy RME resting state unchanged (capability OFF, admission EMPTY, wave None). **Decisive proof — post-merge push run `32243680080` (event `push`, the trigger that previously ran the suite): `NSF-R011 Critical Test Gate: success` AND `NSF-R011 Full Suite Gate: skipped`, with `full_suite_defer_reason=TEMPORARY_FULL_SUITE_POLICY_ACTIVE`** — i.e. every precondition for running it was met and only the policy stopped it; all other gates in that run passed, so no other CI was disabled. **CI infra gotcha (NOT this sprint):** `Install and verify Poppler` (`apt-get install poppler-utils`) hung twice of four attempts (110+ min / 43 min vs **73 s** healthy) with zero tests started — cancel + `gh run rerun` on the same SHA clears it; it warrants its own `timeout-minutes`/caching fix. Also: the critical gate's healthy duration is now ~22–38 min (runner variance), so **duration alone is not a health signal** — check which *step* is in progress; and recent "4–9 min" critical runs are docs-only pushes where the test step was `skipped`, never a valid baseline.

**Posture — the policy is NOT retired by this sprint.** `GLOBAL TEMPORARY FULL-SUITE POLICY = ACTIVE`; `FULL_SUITE_STATUS = SKIPPED_BY_GLOBAL_TEMPORARY_POLICY`; `FULL_SUITE_EXECUTION_COUNT = 0`. Its GO tag `ci-temp-full-suite-schedule-gate-go` attests only to the **gating mechanism** — it does **not** mean the consolidated Full Suite passed, and no deferred fix sprint (e.g. `FIX-LEGACY-RME-ROUTINE-OPS-1`) may be tagged on the strength of it.

## FIX-CLINIC-OPS-BRANCH-CONTEXT-WA-1 — Clinic/Cashier Working Branch, Document Identity, WhatsApp Prescriptions, Super-Admin SATUSEHAT (2026-08-20)

Branch `feature/fix-clinic-ops-branch-context-wa-1` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report` @ `7f28fdf`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/fix-clinic-ops-branch-context-wa-1.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/108-clinic-ops-working-branch-context.mdc`; manifest `.sprint/current.yml` (FOUNDATION_SPRINT). Nine integrated fixes. **One additive migration** (`trx_rme_prescription_whatsapp_deliveries`); no destructive change; ledger untouched; KTP/NIK never rendered or transmitted.

**Shared foundation (FIX-03/04/09).** New canonical `App\Modules\RmeOnlineContext\Services\RmeWorkingBranchScope` — the single answer to "which RME branches may this workspace read?". **Context-bound roles = Admin Klinik, Perawat and now Kasir**, each pinned to the branch selected via the branch-only online context; **fail closed to an EMPTY scope** without one (never MAIN, never first-active, never the whole estate). Governance roles (Owner/Super Admin/Supervisor RME) and the reporting roles keep the full active RME set. **Doctor is deliberately excluded** — the practice-branch model + `DoctorClinicalBranchResolver` are a separate domain and are not regressed. A request `branch_id` may only **narrow**, never widen. Kasir gains `UserOnlineContext::ROLE_KASIR` + `requiresKasirContext`/`isKasirActive`/`startKasirSession` + route `POST rme/online-context/kasir` (`rme.online-context.kasir`), mirroring the RME-BRANCH-SUN4 Perawat precedent. Wired through the pre-existing chokepoints rather than per controller: `ClinicVisitService::scopeBranchIds` (visit list, queue, worklist and **every count widget**), the two policies' single branch predicate (`ClinicVisitPolicy` view/update/transition/print; `RmeInvoicePolicy` view/pay/viewReceipt), `RmeInvoiceService` handoff + pending list, `RmeInvoiceController::rmeBranches()` (drives the receivables list, totals **and CSV export**), and `RmeReportController` — whose patient/payment reports previously applied **no branch predicate at all** unless a `branch_id` was supplied. `RmePaymentService::assertInvoicePayable` (the shared chokepoint of `pay`/`allocateControlPayment`/`allocateVisitPayment`) now also asserts the cashier's working branch.

**FIX-01 — document identity.** `mst_branches.address`/`.phone` existed since the first migration and were already validated — the Master Data form simply had no inputs, so they could never be set. **No migration needed.** The real defect: the Telkomas street address was hardcoded into the **shared** odontogram print template (`structured-print-template.blade.php`), so Landak/Antang/Sunu odontograms printed Telkomas's address. Documents now read the **owning record's** branch (never the viewer's context), and a branch with no address prints **no** address line instead of falling back to `'TELKOMAS'`/`'Makassar'`. Receipt now carries the invoice branch's address.

**FIX-02 — WhatsApp Business prescription delivery.** Server-to-server via Meta's official Cloud API (`POST {base}/{version}/{PHONE_NUMBER_ID}/messages`, Bearer auth). **No `wa.me`, no WhatsApp Web, no browser redirect.** Verified against Meta's current docs: **template messages are the only type deliverable outside an open 24-hour customer service window**, so a proactive hand-off always sends an **approved utility template** — never bypassed. Gateway mirrors the SATUSEHAT pattern (`WhatsAppGatewayInterface` + `Disabled`/`Fake`/`CloudApi`), **OFF by default** — without credentials the disabled gateway is bound and **opens no socket**. Host allowlist + HTTPS enforced **before** any request (SSRF), redirects never followed, access token never logged/persisted/returned. Explicit confirmed operator action gated by new `send_prescription_whatsapp` (Doctor, Supervisor RME); idempotent per prescription+recipient+template; **fails closed** — rejection/timeout/missing credential leaves the clinical record untouched. Config keys documented in the environment example file. **Production readiness: BLOCKED on the owner supplying Meta credentials + an approved template** — production had none, so the feature ships switched off and says so in the UI.

**FIX-05 — examination completion.** New `complete_rme_examination` capability split out of the broad `manage_clinic_visits` (granted Doctor/Perawat/Supervisor RME; **not** Admin Klinik). Admin Klinik keeps registration + room placement but can never close an examination — enforced at the route **and** again in `ClinicVisitService::transitionStatus` so a non-HTTP caller cannot either. The cashier-owned `cashier_pending → completed` path is unchanged.

**FIX-06 — clinical today.** Daftar Kunjungan defaults to the clinical day with explicit `visit_date` / `date_from` / `date_to` history filters ("explicit" = the request carries a date key; clearing the field deliberately shows all). **This exposed a real latent bug:** the filter used `ClinicalClock` (Asia/Makassar) while registration stamped `visit_date` from a **UTC `today()`** — they differ for 8h every day, so a visit registered 00:00–08:00 WITA got **yesterday's** date, queue number and visit number. Both sides now use `ClinicalClock`; `ClinicVisitFactory` follows production; new `clinicalToday()` Pest helper. **Residual, reported not fixed:** `StoreClinicVisitRequest` still falls back to UTC `today()` for RM-number composition when `registered_at` is absent — same class, left alone deliberately because it touches RM composition.

**FIX-07 — read-only visit detail.** New `ClinicVisitPolicy::operateFromDetail` (false only for a front-office-only Admin Klinik). Admin Klinik's visit detail exposes **only Cetak RME**; transition/edit/room-selector/follow-up/RM/odontogram/prescription controls are hidden AND independently denied server-side. Room placement remains available from Antrian Pasien. Deliberately **not** gated on `manage_clinic_visits` so a legitimate viewer (e.g. prescription viewer) keeps its previous access.

**FIX-08 — SATUSEHAT Super Admin only.** One `Gate::define('satusehat.access', …)` in `RepositoryServiceProvider` backs BOTH the route group (`can:satusehat.access` on all 89 `satusehat.*` routes) and the sidebar, so menu and boundary cannot drift. Per-feature SATUSEHAT permissions are **deliberately left in place** — they still tier what a Super Admin may do and some gate non-SATUSEHAT clinical-diagnosis routes. 27 existing SATUSEHAT tests were inverted to the new rule (previously-permitted roles now assert 403 while their permission sets are still asserted); one policy-based candidate-detail IDOR assertion became unobservable over HTTP (Super Admin bypasses policies via `Gate::before`) and its coverage moved to direct policy assertions with a `// FIX-08:` note.

**Also fixed (found by this sprint's own tests):** `EnsureRmeOnlineContext`'s early-return did not know about Kasir, so a cashier with no working context fell straight through to the workspace.

**Tests:** 46 new across `FixClinicOpsWorkingBranchContextTest` (18), `FixClinicOpsVisitActionsTest` (9), `FixClinicOpsBranchDocumentIdentityTest` (5), `PrescriptionWhatsAppDeliveryTest` (15). Existing tests updated **for** the new rules, not around them — Kasir fixtures select a working branch (`rmeMakeKasirActive`) exactly as Perawat's did; examination-completing actors hold the capability. No test skipped, deleted or weakened. Deploy: `php artisan migrate --force` + `db:seed --class=PermissionSeeder --force` + `db:seed --class=RoleSeeder --force` + `permission:cache-reset`; keep `WHATSAPP_ENABLED=false` until Meta credentials and an approved template exist.

**Closure (2026-08-20) — WATCH, no GO tag.** PR #321 → runtime `99c5643` (deployed; VPS HEAD exact match, working tree clean). Follow-up **PR #322 → `82f1122` is TEST-ONLY** and deliberately NOT deployed — runtime authority stays `99c5643`. **Two authorised consolidated Full Suites** ran under the still-ACTIVE GLOBAL TEMPORARY FULL-SUITE POLICY: run `32319351675` on `99c5643` → **1 failed** / 30283 assertions / 14172.78s, and run `32344343470` on `82f1122` → **0 failed** / 30284 assertions / 14309.25s with the `Run full Pest suite` step confirmed **executed** (classifier `full_suite_authorized=true`, `gate_profile=unknown_high_risk`) and every other gate green (Quality, Classifier, Critical, Selective Module, NSF-9, NSF-10). The first run's only failure was Sprint 41's blanket "no WhatsApp send route anywhere" assertion, superseded by FIX-02; #322 **narrowed** it rather than deleting it — the receivable/follow-up surface still asserts no send route of its own, and the app-wide permitted set is now pinned exactly to `['rme.prescriptions.whatsapp.send']` (stricter than the old substring ban; verified against production runtime — 566 routes, exactly one send route). **FIX-02 remains BLOCKED_EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY:** production carries no access token, no phone number ID and no approved utility template, so `DisabledWhatsAppGateway` is bound and no socket opens. Re-verified against Meta's current docs on 2026-08-20 — templates are still the only type deliverable outside an open 24-hour window, and Graph v23.0 is supported to 8 Oct 2027 (latest v26.0); since the graph version and all credentials come from configuration, **activation is config-only**, so this Full Suite result stays valid once credentials exist. Production re-verified at closure: `/login` + all three health endpoints 200, guest surfaces 302 (no 500), pilot/debug-off/maintenance-off, Legacy RME `AT_REST`, zero new application errors. **Known defect, reported not fixed:** `asia_dental_lab`, a 2.9 MB **SQLite file committed to the repository root** by this merge, is schema-only (only `migrations`/`sqlite_sequence` populated; every patient/user/clinical/invoice/payment/audit table empty → **zero PII**, repo hygiene not a security incident). It stems from `DB_DATABASE=asia_dental_lab` plus a SQLite connection and no ignore rule covers it; it was left alone so a new commit could not move the source tip out from under the authorised Full Suite. Follow-up PR should untrack it and ignore `*.sqlite`/`*.sqlite3`.

## FIX-RME-CONSENT-WORKFLOW-PRINT-UX-2 — signed consent as the payment gate, RME page ownership, print ownership, one-page kwitansi (2026-08-20)

Branch `feature/fix-rme-consent-workflow-print-ux-2` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report` @ `2b1725c`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/fix-rme-consent-workflow-print-ux-2.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/100-rme-consent-payment-gate-print-ownership.mdc`; manifest `.sprint/current.yml` (FOUNDATION_SPRINT — cross-module, the stricter classification). **One additive migration** (`trx_rme_visit_consents`); two new permissions; no destructive migration; ledger/SATUSEHAT/Legacy RME/WhatsApp untouched (FIX-CLINIC-OPS FIX-02 stays HOLD / BLOCKED_EXTERNAL_DEPENDENCY — gateway, config and credentials not touched).

**FIX-01 — RME payment now requires a signed PERSETUJUAN TINDAKAN MEDIS.** The defect closed: there was **no consent document at all**, only two booleans on `trx_clinic_visits` **that the payment request itself supplied** — `RmePaymentService::applyConsentVerification()` wrote `$data['consent_signed_by_patient'|'..._doctor']` onto the visit and `assertConsentVerified()` read back the row it had just written, in all three entry points (`pay`, `allocateControlPayment`, `allocateVisitPayment`). A POST of `consent_signed_by_patient=1&consent_signed_by_doctor=1` settled an invoice with no document, no signature and no patient involvement. `applyConsentVerification()` is **deleted**; the assertion is now a pure read of persisted evidence. New bounded context `App\Modules\Consent` (Controller → FormRequest → Service → RepositoryInterface → Repository → Model) + `config/rme_consent.php` holding the clinic's printed wording, **snapshotted into every signed consent** so template edits cannot rewrite history. Signing is possible **only at `cashier_pending`** (earlier = consent to an undecided treatment, later = back-dating). **[SUPERSEDED by FIX-RME-EXAM-CONSENT-ODONTOGRAM-HISTORY-3 / FIX-02: consent is signable for any NON-TERMINAL visit and is taken at the start of the examination.]** Signature evidence reuses `PrescriptionCanvasDecoder` (real PNG, non-blank, **pure PHP — no GD**). Doctor comes from the visit, never the request; `Saya sendiri` copies the canonical patient identity. **Clause 8 (documentation/publication) is an explicit YA/TIDAK with no default, validated `present|boolean` (NOT `required`, which rejects `0`), and answering TIDAK never blocks treatment or payment.** Signed consent is **immutable** — no update path, policy hard-denies `update`/`delete`; correction is void-with-reason + re-sign and the voided row is kept. Signatures on a **private disk**, no public URL, streamed only through the policy; identity numbers + signature paths are `$hidden`; the consent document is the ONE surface that renders a full identity number (the form legally records "No KTP/SIM"), permission-gated and branch-scoped. **New permissions `manage_rme_consents` / `view_rme_consents`** (Doctor, Kasir, Admin Klinik, Supervisor RME) — deliberately NOT `manage_clinic_visits`, which **Kasir does not hold**, so reusing it would have 403'd the one role that must complete every payment (consent routes sit inside the RME group, which already requires `view_clinic_visits|manage_clinic_visits`; a real-Kasir test pins this). The legacy booleans are kept as a **display-only** record that a paper form was sighted, so pre-sprint visits keep a truthful history: `hasVerifiedConsent()` = display (document OR attestation), `hasSignedConsentDocument()` = the gate. **Operational consequence:** a visit at `cashier_pending` at deploy time cannot be paid until consent is taken — surfaced on the visit detail and payment page, so the cashier never discovers it by being refused. **[SUPERSEDED by FIX-RME-EXAM-CONSENT-ODONTOGRAM-HISTORY-3 / FIX-03: consent is no longer a payment condition at all, so no visit is ever unpayable for lack of consent.]** **Gate boundary (found by adversarial review, blocked merge until fixed) [SUPERSEDED by FIX-RME-EXAM-CONSENT-ODONTOGRAM-HISTORY-3 / FIX-03 — the payment gate is REMOVED entirely; the reasoning below is retained because it explains why historical receivables must stay collectible]:** consent gates TREATMENT, not debt collection — it is required only while the visit is at `cashier_pending`, NOT for instalments on an already-`completed` visit nor for carry-over receivables. The partial-payment rule completes a visit on its first payment while the invoice stays `PARTIAL` and payable, so "visit completed + invoice PARTIAL" is the normal shape of an outstanding instalment and of every pre-sprint receivable; since consent cannot be signed for a completed visit, gating every `pay()` made the entire existing receivables book permanently uncollectable on a path the Piutang screen links to. The exemption is safe only because `completed` is not an accepted `TransitionStatusRequest` value, `transitionStatus()` refuses it from anywhere but `cashier_pending`, and `RmeInvoiceService::create()` refuses a non-`cashier_pending` visit — all three asserted, not assumed. **Performance:** `hasVerifiedConsent()` was a free column read and is now a query, so the unpaginated cashier handoff board eager-loads `consents`.

**FIX-02 — Medical Record hierarchy** is now `Informasi Kunjungan → RME Tulisan Tangan → Dokumen RME Pasien` (the documents rail used to render above the visit info). Supersedes LEGACY-RME-DOCTOR-WORKSPACE-1's "rail above the history card"; safe because **1A inlined the published legacy pages into the handwriting canvas itself**, so the archive is still near the top — a new test pins that safety net.

**FIX-03 — Finalisasi** moved into the page-header actions. **Moved, not duplicated** (asserted by counting both the route and the label); authorisation, route and the mandatory-handwriting precondition unchanged; its state messages travel with it.

**FIX-04 — Cetak RME** moved from Detail Kunjungan to the Medical Record page header, mirroring `Cetak Odontogram`, reusing the **existing** print route (no second endpoint). It prints `$clinicVisit`, which `MedicalRecordController` binds to `$activeSheet->clinicVisit` — the visit whose sheet is on screen. **Supersedes FIX-CLINIC-OPS (FIX-07)** "Admin Klinik's visit detail is read-only plus Cetak RME": the front office would otherwise have *lost* the capability (the Rekam Medis card is behind `$canOperateVisit`), so it gets a **navigation link** — not a second print button — gated by the same ability that decides whether it may print; clinicians already have the card and get no duplicate.

**FIX-05 — the odontogram is out of the RME print.** Removed from `partials/print-body.blade.php`, which both `print.blade.php` and `print-pdf.blade.php` include, so one edit covers browser print and PDF; the dead status-label maps, the unused `$odontogramPrint` view-model and its eager load went with it. **Composition only** — model, table, workflow, editor UI and standalone print untouched. `MedicalRecordPrintOdontogramSeparationTest` proves both halves, the PDF half against real dompdf output extracted with `pdftotext` plus positive controls so the absence checks cannot pass vacuously.

**FIX-06 — the kwitansi is one measurable page.** Root cause: the receipt declared **no `@page` rule at all** — the only print template in the repo that did not — so margins were the browser default. Now A4 + 10mm with compacted card chrome/logo/table padding. One page by removing waste, never by hiding money: nothing clipped, no row suppressed, totals kept whole, and beyond the supported envelope the document **continues** with a repeating header rather than truncating. Because a print dialog cannot be measured, the receipt is also renderable through the existing dompdf pipeline — new route `rme.cashier.receipt.pdf` sharing the receipt's authorisation (`viewReceipt`), its PAID-only rule and, via a new `resolveReceiptData()`, its data, so the two cannot drift. Tests render the real PDF, read the page count with **poppler `pdfinfo`** (already a CI-verified dependency) and then extract the text to assert every item, subtotal, total, payment and identity survives. **Recorded, not changed:** the kwitansi is a *pelunasan* document — both receipt routes redirect unless the invoice is PAID, so a partial payment has no receipt at all.

**Superseded contracts updated in place with the reason recorded inline (never worked around):** `MedicalRecordPrintOdontogramMergeTest` → replaced by `MedicalRecordPrintOdontogramSeparationTest`; `StructuredOdontogramPrintTemplateTest`, `RmePdfPrintHardeningTest`, `ClinicVisitTest` (×3), `OdontogramSelectedResultsTableTest`, `RmeOdontogramUixTest` (odontogram/print); `FixClinicOpsVisitActionsTest` (Admin Klinik); `LegacyRmeDoctorWorkspaceDocumentsTest` (rail ordering); `RmePatientFormatConsentTest` (×4), `RmeDoctorCashierCompletionGateTest`, `CashierPaymentUixTest` (consent). **Test-infra:** `tests/Pest.php` gains `rmeSignedConsentFor()` (payment-mechanics fixtures) plus `pdfPageCount()`/`pdfExtractText()`/`pdfToTextAvailable()`/`pdfInfoAvailable()` reusing poppler; tests about consent itself must go through `RmeVisitConsentService` so the rules stay under test. New suites `RmeVisitConsentGateTest` (26), `RmeVisitConsentHttpTest` (17), `RmeReceiptOnePageTest` (9), `MedicalRecordPageActionsTest` (12), `MedicalRecordPrintOdontogramSeparationTest` (10) — all five added to the CI critical filter in **both** gate variants.

Deploy: `php artisan migrate --force` + `db:seed --class=PermissionSeeder --force` + `db:seed --class=RoleSeeder --force` + `permission:cache-reset`.

Candidate `39318f7`; base `2b1725c`.

## FIX-RME-EXAM-CONSENT-ODONTOGRAM-HISTORY-3 — explicit examination completion, consent as the RME authoring gate, cashier consent independence, patient odontogram history + Legacy Odontogram (2026-08-21)

Branch `feature/fix-rme-exam-consent-odontogram-history-3` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, baseline `fix-rme-consent-workflow-print-ux-2-go` @ runtime `b080ab1` / base tip `ac6d620`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/fix-rme-exam-consent-odontogram-history-3.md`; durable rules `.cursor/rules/109-rme-exam-consent-odontogram-history.mdc`. **GLOBAL TEMPORARY FULL-SUITE POLICY stays ACTIVE — `FULL_SUITE_EXECUTION_COUNT=0`, Full Suite DEFERRED, never reported as passed.**

**The canonical RME workflow is now:** `Pendaftaran → Antrian → Input Ruangan → Dokter "Mulai Pemeriksaan" (in_progress) → Persetujuan Tindakan Medis (unlocks RME authoring) → RME + Odontogram → RME/odontogram complete = STILL in_progress → Dokter "Selesai Pemeriksaan" (cashier_pending) → Kasir / Pembayaran (no consent check) → completed.` Historical clinical evidence (RME history, Legacy RME, native odontogram history, Legacy Odontogram) is read-only and readable throughout; the **active odontogram workflow is unchanged**.

**FIX-01 — a clinical document never completes an examination.** `MedicalRecordService::finalize()` used to transition an `in_progress` visit to `cashier_pending`, so finishing a DOCUMENT silently ended the EXAMINATION. The audit confirmed it was the ONLY implicit path (no observer, event, listener, job, scheduled command or model hook writes visit status; the odontogram module never touches it). The call is deleted and **`ClinicVisitService` is no longer a constructor dependency of `MedicalRecordService`**, so the class cannot transition a visit even by accident. The explicit action already existed with correct authority and needed no change: `POST rme/visits/{visit}/transition` → `ClinicVisitPolicy::completeExamination` / `complete_rme_examination` (Doctor, Perawat, Supervisor RME; Super Admin via `Gate::before`) — Admin Klinik and Kasir were already denied. A **"Selesai Pemeriksaan" button was added to the Medical Record page header** pointing at that same guarded route: without it a doctor could finalize, leave, and strand the patient at `in_progress` with the cashier unable to raise an invoice, because `RmeInvoiceService::create()` still requires `cashier_pending`.

**FIX-02 — consent is the RME AUTHORING gate, not the payment gate.** **Consent timing (CORRECTIVE-01):** `RmeVisitConsentService::isSignable()` is **`in_progress` ONLY** — consent becomes actionable once the authorized doctor explicitly clicks "Mulai Pemeriksaan". It is refused at `registered`/`waiting` (no treatment decided yet, so a signature would consent to nothing) and at `cashier_pending`/`completed`/`cancelled` (recording agreement after the fact, or back-dating). Enforced server-side, not by hiding a button. **Authoring authority (CORRECTIVE-02) is POSITIVE, never absence-of-blocker:** `assertRmeAuthoringAllowedForPatient()` must establish from server state that (1) the patient HAS a current encounter, (2) it is `in_progress`, (3) the actor may work on it (working-branch + doctor clinical scope), and (4) a valid signed consent exists for THAT SAME encounter. "No encounter at all" fails closed. The earlier shape asked "does this patient have an open UNCONSENTED encounter?" and allowed the write when the answer was no — which had two reproducers, **cancel-then-author** and **complete-then-author** (closing the live visit removed the blocker and the canonical record became writable again). Both are now impossible and pinned. New read-only primitive `App\Modules\ClinicVisit\Services\ActiveEncounterResolver` answers the one question a caller cannot influence; **authority may not come from `medical_record.clinic_visit_id`** (Sprint 64.0.2 makes that the patient's first, long-finished visit) **nor from the request** (`{clinicVisit}`, `source_visit_id`, `?sheet=`). Enforced in the SERVICE layer (`createDraft`/`updateDraft`/`finalize`/`getOrCreateCanonicalMedicalRecord`, `MedicalRecordDiagnosisService`, `DiagnosisRolloutService::grantOverride`) plus `MedicalRecordHandwritingController`. **Sprint 59 effect:** for WRITES this supersedes "any visit is freely editable at any time"; its actual subject — a *finalized* record is not frozen — is preserved, since during an authorized consented encounter a finalized record is still editable. **Reads are never gated** (history, legacy archive, previous odontograms, the record itself), including when there is no active encounter. **[The "active odontogram is deliberately NOT consent-gated" decision is SUPERSEDED by CORRECTIVE-03 below.]**

**CORRECTIVE-03 — the ACTIVE ODONTOGRAM is gated too, and finishing the examination needs consent.** Realtime verification against the pilot found the workflow still wrong. Two gaps were genuine; the rest were the same gaps' symptoms plus the fact that **none of this sprint had been deployed** — production was still on the previous sprint (`b080ab1`), so FIX-01..FIX-04 were simply not live. (1) **The active odontogram had no consent gate.** That "explicit product decision" does not survive contact with the clinic: a doctor could chart every tooth, diagnosis and planned treatment on an unconsented patient while only the prose note beside it was refused. `OdontogramService::updatePlaceholder()`/`::finalize()` now assert `RmeVisitConsentService::assertOdontogramAuthoringAllowed()`. Because an odontogram row belongs to ONE visit — unlike handwriting, which Sprint 64.0.2 stores on the patient's canonical record — this gate is **stricter and exact**: the chart must BE the live encounter's chart. That is what makes a previous visit's chart **permanently read-only**, and a consent signed today never reopens it; it narrows Sprint 59 for the odontogram exactly as CORRECTIVE-02 did for the record, while the finalization lock stays gone on the live encounter. **Reading is untouched** — the page still opens and still creates its empty placeholder row, because the doctor must SEE the chart to decide the treatment being consented to, and an empty draft with no recorded teeth is not a clinical finding. (2) **"Selesai Pemeriksaan" did not require consent**, so an unsigned `in_progress` visit could be pushed to `cashier_pending`. That is not a missing check but an **unrecoverable state**: CORRECTIVE-01 makes consent signable only at `in_progress`, so once the visit moves on it can never be consented — the record can never be written, yet the treatment arrives at the cashier as a bill. `ClinicVisitService::transitionStatus()` now refuses `→ cashier_pending` without a valid signed consent, in the SERVICE so a command, job or another service cannot close an examination the patient never agreed to. The audit confirmed the only other `transitionStatus()` callers are `RmePaymentService`'s three `→ completed` calls, which are unaffected. **Test-fixture consequence:** a fixture that intends to WRITE clinical data must build a real encounter — `rmeActiveConsentedEncounter($visit)` / `rmeConsentedOdontogramVisit([...])` in `tests/Pest.php`; `rmeSignedConsentFor()` alone authorises nothing, because a consent on a queued or finished visit is not a live encounter. Pinned by `tests/Feature/RME/RmeExamConsentCorrective3Test.php` (21) covering the RME, odontogram, history, no-auto-completion, explicit-completion and cashier matrices, and verified on **PostgreSQL 16.14** (production's version) as well as sqlite.

**FIX-03 — consent is not a cashier concern.** `assertConsentVerified()` and its three call sites are deleted; `CreateRmePaymentRequest` accepts no consent field; the payment page's consent block, the `CONSENT_PENDING` handoff rung and the cashier queue's Consent column are removed (9 `<th>` / 9 `<td>` verified). The safety property is **stronger**, not weaker: a treatment that was recorded at all already had consent, whereas the old gate only fired while the visit was at `cashier_pending`, so every carry-over receivable and every instalment on an already-completed visit bypassed it by design. Historical receivables stay collectible. The legacy `consent_signed_by_*` columns and `hasVerifiedConsent()` remain as historical display only — nothing reads them for authorization, and the display surfaces were switched to `hasSignedConsentDocument()` so they do not report "Belum" forever. **UI governance rule updated honestly:** `x-ui.alert` is no longer required on the cashier payment view, because the alert it required was the consent gate — a component-adoption rule must not force a page to keep a banner whose subject moved.

**FIX-04 — patient odontogram history + Legacy Odontogram.** New read-only **"Riwayat Odontogram Pasien"** card below the active chart and **outside** the `x-data="odontogramEditor(...)"` wrapper (so its per-row Alpine state can never bind against the chart being edited). New `OdontogramRepositoryInterface::patientHistoryForBranches()` — branch ids are a REQUIRED parameter and an empty set fails closed; the query joins `trx_clinic_visits` so every column is table-qualified (both tables have `branch_id`) and adds `whereNull('trx_clinic_visits.deleted_at')` because a join does not inherit the model's soft-delete scope. `OdontogramService::patientHistoryForVisit()` applies the RME branch set **and** `DoctorPatientScopeService`, excludes the current visit and excludes empty auto-created drafts, and orders by the owning **visit's clinical date**. **`OdontogramPolicy::update` is UNCHANGED** — it still answers only the role/branch/patient-scope question, so a refusal carries a clear reason instead of a bare 403, and the history SURFACE stays read-only by construction rather than by hiding a button. **[The claim that a doctor may still correct a PAST chart is SUPERSEDED by CORRECTIVE-03: a previous visit's chart is permanently read-only. Correction of a closed encounter is no longer a doctor self-service action.]** New bounded context `App\Modules\LegacyOdontogram` (35 files, 4 additive tables `stg_odontogram_legacy_imports` / `_pages` / `trx_odontogram_legacy_records` / `_pages`, its own flag `rme.legacy_odontogram_archive` default OFF, its own private disk `legacy_odontogram_private`, 6 permissions granted to NO role, 13 routes) — immutable published evidence (VOID + re-import), branch derived from the RM number and never submitted, manually chosen date validated by `ClinicalClock` strictly before the patient's earliest **native odontogram** (a dedicated `PatientEarliestNativeOdontogramDateResolver` — reusing the RME resolver would key on the earliest visit owning a MEDICAL RECORD, a different fact), PDF-only v1, policy-gated private streaming, flag gates migration only (reading a published archive is never flag-gated). **It does NOT touch the Legacy RME tables, wave, quota, admission or capability state**, adds no discriminator column to the Legacy RME published tables, and uses its own `LegacyOdontogramWorkspaceScope` rather than widening `LegacyRmeWorkspaceScope::GOVERNANCE_PERMISSIONS`.

**Superseded by CORRECTIVE-03 (within this sprint):** its own earlier "the active odontogram workflow is unchanged / NOT consent-gated" decision, and Sprint 59's "any odontogram is revisable at any time" as it applies to a CLOSED encounter.

**Superseded:** `.cursor/rules/100` §1 ("consent is the RME payment gate"), §1a ("required while the visit is at `cashier_pending`") and §2 ("signable only at `cashier_pending`"); `docs/ai-knowledge/08` ("Finalize RM → transition ke cashier_pending"). **Preserved unchanged:** `.cursor/rules/100` §1b and §3–11; `.cursor/rules/108` §10–12 (examination-completion authority, cashier-owned `completed`, "hiding a button is never the boundary"). Legacy RME programme state and the WhatsApp HOLD are untouched.

## POST-RME-ODONTOGRAM-STABILIZATION-1 — odontogram view creates no clinical row, repository SQLite artifact hygiene, RM number on the clinical calendar (2026-08-21)

Branch `feature/post-rme-odontogram-stabilization-1` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report` @ `7eb959b1` = GO tag `fix-rme-exam-consent-odontogram-history-3-go`, the exact production runtime; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/post-rme-odontogram-stabilization-1.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/110-post-rme-odontogram-stabilization.mdc`. **No migration, no permission, no new status, no clinical workflow redesign.** The Pendaftaran → Antrian → Input Ruangan → Pemeriksaan → Consent → Selesai Pemeriksaan → Kasir authority is unchanged and re-pinned by tests.

**FIX-01 — opening the odontogram no longer creates a clinical row.** `OdontogramController::show()` called `OdontogramService::getOrCreateForVisit()`, so every `GET /rme/visits/{clinicVisit}/odontogram` INSERTed a `trx_odontograms` row. Two aggravations: the route is gated `view_clinic_visits|manage_clinic_visits` and the controller authorized `create` (which only needs `canView()`), so a **read-only operator provably wrote a row**; and `getOrCreateForVisit()` never consulted `RmeVisitConsentService`, so **a row was created even when consent forbade authoring**. The codebase had already grown two workarounds instead of removing the cause (`hasRecordedTeeth()` filtering empty drafts out of history, and `whereNotNull('tooth_map_payload')` in `patientHistoryForBranches()`). **Not a one-line deletion:** creation-on-view was the ONLY path in the entire HTTP surface that created a native odontogram — the save form's action *is* the odontogram id (`route('rme.odontograms.update', $odontogram)`) and update/finalize/print are all model-bound. Changes: new `OdontogramService::findForVisit()` (pure read) + `draftForVisit()` (saved chart, else an **unsaved** in-memory instance — chosen over `null` because `OdontogramPrintFormatter::format()`, `dmftCounts()` and the view are all typed non-nullable) + `saveForVisit()` (create-on-first-write, **asserts consent BEFORE the insert** so a refused write leaves no trace); `getOrCreateForVisit()` retained as the create primitive but unreachable from the read path; new controller `store()`; new `OdontogramPolicy::author(User, ClinicVisit)` requiring `canManage()` (the write-level counterpart of the view-level `create`); new route `PATCH rme/visits/{clinicVisit}/odontogram` → `rme.visits.odontogram.store` **inside the existing `permission:manage_clinic_visits` + `visit.room` group** (so the Sprint 60.8 room gate covers a first chart exactly as it covers a revision — `EnsureVisitRoomAssigned::resolveVisit()` already prefers the `{clinicVisit}` param); Blade branches form action / print button / finalize button on `$odontogram->exists` (an unsaved model has no route key and `route()` would throw). **Deliberate behaviour changes:** the print button is hidden until a chart is saved (the print route is model-bound, so it could only 404 before); and SATUSEHAT dental readiness moves an uncharted visit from the blocking `DENTAL_MAPPING_BLOCKED` (empty row ⇒ `odontogram_present=true` with 0 supported resources) to the non-blocking informational `dental_odontogram_missing` — removing false blocking, weakening no gate. **Known coupling deliberately NOT changed:** `LegacyOdontogramNativeReferenceRepository::earliestVisitWithOdontogramForPatient()` uses `whereHas('odontogram')` and still counts pre-existing empty rows; rows already in production are untouched so no existing patient's Legacy Odontogram import cutoff moves, and narrowing that resolver would change admissibility for existing clinical data — an owner decision and a separate sprint.

**FIX-02 — the tracked SQLite artifact at the repository root.** A 2,924,544-byte file `asia_dental_lab`, added by PR #321 (`2913c4a`). **Re-audited, not inherited:** genuine SQLite 3, **schema-only** — 129 tables, only `migrations` (167 rows) and `sqlite_sequence` (25) populated; `mst_patients` / `users` / `mst_doctors` / `trx_clinic_visits` / `trx_medical_records` / `trx_odontograms` / `sys_audit_logs` / `sessions` / `notifications` / `trx_rme_payments` / `trx_rme_invoices` all **0 rows**; no secret (`password`/`token` appear only as DDL column names); stale (167 recorded vs 172 migration files). **Not a security incident.** **Root cause:** `config/database.php:37` resolves the sqlite file from `DB_DATABASE` (`env('DB_DATABASE', database_path('database.sqlite'))`), and a local environment file legitimately holds the *PostgreSQL database name* `asia_dental_lab` — so any command run with `DB_CONNECTION=sqlite` resolves that name as a **relative path** and writes a database into the working directory. Fix: `git rm --cached` (file left on disk, no history rewrite) + an **anchored** `/asia_dental_lab` ignore rule (it cannot be `*.sqlite`/`*.db` — the file has no extension; `database/.gitignore` already covers the legitimate `database/database.sqlite`) + the real guard `tests/Feature/Architecture/RepositoryArtifactHygieneTest.php`, which detects by the `SQLite format 3\0` **header** so it catches a future stray under any name, and which was **negative-controlled** (force-add the artifact → the test fails). Every `asia_dental_lab` / `asia_dental_lab_pilot` PostgreSQL **database name** (environment example, `config/ci_runner.php`, `scripts/backup_postgres.sh`, `scripts/restore_postgres.sh`, docs) is untouched and pinned by a test. **Rejected as over-broad:** `force="true"` on phpunit.xml's `DB_DATABASE` would override the `DB_DATABASE: testing`/`daengtisia_ci` exported by six CI job blocks while `DB_CONNECTION` stays `pgsql`, breaking every DB-touching test in every gate.

**FIX-03 — RM number and registration date now follow the clinical calendar.** This is exactly the residual FIX-CLINIC-OPS-BRANCH-CONTEXT-WA-1 recorded and declined to fix (`docs/sprints/fix-clinic-ops-branch-context-wa-1.md` "## Known residual" — *"It is reported, not fixed."*). `config/app.php:68` hard-codes `'timezone' => 'UTC'` **on purpose** (technical instants stay UTC; `config/clinical.php` is the separate `Asia/Makassar` authority via `ClinicalClock`), so a raw `Carbon::today()` is the UTC calendar day — and for the first **eight hours of every clinical day** the UTC date is yesterday, which on 1 January is the previous **year**, baked permanently into the Nomor RM. **The residual was broader than reported — five sites**, not one: `PatientMedicalRecordNumberService::composeForRegistration()` (`Carbon::now()` fallback), `PatientService::resolveRegisteredAt()` (**the persisting site**), `StorePatientRequest`, `UpdatePatientRequest`, and the reported `StoreClinicVisitRequest`. Two consequences: a patient registered 07:00 WITA on 1 Jan 2027 got `DG-{BRANCH}-2026-…` with `registered_at = 2026-12-31`; and **one registration produced two different years**, because `ClinicVisitService` already used `ClinicalClock` for `visit_date` (FIX-06) while RM composition did not. All five now resolve "today" through `ClinicalClock`; a **supplied** date is used verbatim (it is a calendar date — timezone-converting it would corrupt history). `LegacyPatientImportService` untouched (its year comes from the operator-supplied Timestamp column). **RM format, parser, branch authority and every existing identifier are unchanged — no patient is renumbered; the fix is prospective and no reconciliation migration is included.**

**Governance/CI.** An Odontogram-only change classifies as `runtime_app` with no module flag, the Selective Module Gate has no RME step, and `Odontogram` was **absent** from the NSF-R011 critical `--filter` (only `LegacyOdontogram`, which does not match `OdontogramTest`) — so this sprint's own core tests would not have run in CI. `Odontogram` and `RepositoryArtifactHygiene` added to the critical filter in **both** the GitHub-hosted and self-hosted jobs. `config/sprint_regression_matrix.php` already declares category `rme` with `ci_jobs => ['critical_test_gate','selective_module_gate']` but its only consumer is `SprintTestPlanner` — the critical-gate half of that claim is now true; **the `selective_module_gate` half remains unimplemented and is recorded as a known gap, not silently closed.** Full Suite not run: the temporary global policy is ACTIVE and `full_suite_gate` is structurally unreachable on a `pull_request` event.

**Tests.** New `tests/Feature/RME/PostRmeOdontogramStabilizationTest.php` (28 — view-writes-nothing, first-save-creates, no-duplicate, and the adversarial matrix: unsigned crafted store creates **no row**, view-only 403, room gate, cancelled/completed/previous-visit/non-RME-branch all refused with no row); new `tests/Feature/RME/PostRmeRmNumberClinicalClockTest.php` (11 — WITA year boundary either side of 16:00 UTC, UTC-midnight-within-one-clinical-day, persisting site, pre-check/persist agreement, supplied-date verbatim, format+parser unchanged); new `tests/Feature/Architecture/RepositoryArtifactHygieneTest.php` (5, incl. a static guard that fails if any file under `app/` outside `OdontogramService` references the consent-free `getOrCreateForVisit()` primitive). Updated: `OdontogramTest` (2 contracts **reversed** — the old tests asserted the defect — plus 1 added and 2 print cases now chart first) and `OdontogramAdditionalFieldsTest` (2 cases moved to the first-save route). **Both new behavioural suites were negative-controlled** so neither is vacuous: the FIX-01 hygiene guard fails when the artifact is force-tracked, and 4 of the 11 FIX-03 boundary tests fail against pre-fix code and pass after. **Test-infra gotcha re-confirmed:** never run two `artisan test` processes in the same worktree — `Storage::fake` wipes the shared disk and manufactures phantom LegacyRme/Pilot failures (2 vs 5 failures across overlapping runs); kill `pestphp/pest/bin/pest` and verify isolation before diagnosing.

**PostgreSQL verification.** SQLite green is not sufficient here — this exact area already produced a production-only defect (comparing the `jsonb` `tooth_map_payload` to a string is a hard PostgreSQL error SQLite never surfaces). Re-run against a real server on a scratch database: `Odontogram|PostRme*|RepositoryArtifactHygiene` **362 passed/1149 assertions**, `Patient|ClinicVisit|LegacyOdontogram|RmeExamConsent|MedicalRecordFinalization` **734 passed/2197 assertions** — **1,096 PostgreSQL tests, 0 failures**. Local server is **18.6**, production is **16.14**; recorded rather than glossed (CI additionally pins a `postgres:16` service). Scratch DB dropped; `asia_dental_lab`/`_test` untouched.

**Adversarial review.** Three independent lenses, each finding adversarially verified: **CRITICAL=0, HIGH=0** — the security lens *"could not construct any path that creates a `trx_odontograms` row without a signed consent"*. Seven LOW findings fixed, notably a **real concurrency race this sprint introduced**: moving creation onto the Save button made a double-submit realistic, and the bare check-then-act would have violated the `UNIQUE` on `clinic_visit_id`, rolled back, and lost the doctor's charted teeth — now decided under `findByClinicVisitForUpdate()` (`lockForUpdate`). Also: `getOrCreateForVisit()` marked `@internal` with its no-production-caller claim now test-enforced; the Legacy Odontogram cutoff shift pinned by 3 tests; rule 110 given an explicit supersession clause over rule 109 §2 plus correction of the two stale code comments carrying the same claim; manifest restored from WATCH to **GO** (`go_tag`/`full_suite_status`/`inherits_*`); `PatientService` moved to constructor injection for consistency.

**Pre-existing failure, NOT from this sprint:** `tests/Unit/Services/Monitoring/PilotPerformanceSnapshotLogAnalyzerTest.php` → *"it keeps overall ok for historical-only grouped stack traces via service"* returns `overall_status` `WATCH` instead of `OK`. Reproduced **identically on a pristine detached worktree at the production SHA `7eb959b1` with zero changes**; environment-dependent (the WATCH originates outside the logs section). Reported, not fixed.

## LEGACY-ODONTOGRAM-NATIVE-REFERENCE-CUTOFF-1 — an empty odontogram placeholder is not clinical evidence for the archive cutoff (2026-08-22)

Branch `feature/legacy-odontogram-native-reference-cutoff-1` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report`, baseline `post-rme-odontogram-stabilization-1-go` @ `9aa45a8`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/legacy-odontogram-native-reference-cutoff-1.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/111-legacy-odontogram-native-reference-cutoff.mdc`. Settles the residual POST-RME-ODONTOGRAM-STABILIZATION-1 deliberately left open. **No migration, no route, no permission, no schema change; no native odontogram row deleted/edited/backfilled; no auto-import/auto-publish/migration batch; Legacy Odontogram stays at rest.**

**The defect.** `LegacyOdontogramNativeReferenceRepository` decided "this patient has a native odontogram" from bare row existence (`->whereHas('odontogram')`), so a row charting nothing counted as clinical evidence. **The cutoff acts in TWO directions** from one `resolve()` call: an eligibility **GATE** (`null` ⇒ `CODE_PATIENT_HAS_NO_NATIVE_ODONTOGRAM`) and a chronological **BOUND** (strictly earlier). A contentless placeholder therefore both opened the gate on evidence that does not exist and drew the bound on a date where nothing was charted. Under a narrowing predicate the two directions move **opposite** ways — always state both.

**Production read-only impact** (`asia_dental_lab_pilot`, PG 16.15, every session under `SET default_transaction_read_only = on`): 32 in-scope rows → **31 meaningful / 1 empty**; 15 patients with native rows → **14 meaningful / 1 empty-only**; `cutoff_moves_LATER` = 0; `WOULD_BECOME_ELIGIBLE_AFTER_FIX` = **0**; `BLOCKED_BY_MEANINGFUL_NATIVE` **14 before == 14 after**; delta **−1** (patient 45, whose sole odontogram is a `draft` with `NULL` payload on a visit with no medical record). **The brief's premise was inverted by the evidence** — an empty row is the only thing that can *open* the GATE, so on the gate direction ignoring it can only refuse more. That is NOT a general monotonicity guarantee: on the BOUND direction, dropping a patient's *earliest* row moves the bound LATER and **widens** the window (pinned by test, clinically correct — nothing was charted on the placeholder's date). `cutoff_moves_LATER = 0` is a measurement of today's data, not a structural property. Archive at rest (0 imports, 0 records), so nothing published was retroactively invalidated.

**Measurement error, caught and retracted.** The first pass used `jsonb_typeof(payload->'teeth')='array'` and reported 0 meaningful / 32 empty / all 15 patients empty-only. **`teeth` is a JSON OBJECT keyed by FDI number, never a list**, so the array test was false everywhere and mis-classified 31 rows of real charting as empty. Caught because the same output self-contradicted (`payload_empty_teeth = 0` while claiming all rows empty). **Verify the payload shape before trusting any count; contradictory numbers in one result mean the predicate is wrong.**

**The predicate already existed** — `private OdontogramService::hasRecordedTeeth()`, reachable only by Patient Odontogram History, while the cutoff counted row existence: two answers to one question. This sprint invented no third. Changes: `Odontogram::hasRecordedTeeth()` promoted onto the model (**the one definition**, beside `dmftCounts()` which reads the same payload); `OdontogramService` delegates (behaviour unchanged); the cutoff repository now requires content via the portable SQL half `whereNotNull('tooth_map_payload')` + the canonical PHP predicate, earliest-first with early exit via `lazy()`; interface contract restated. A charted tooth counts **regardless of status, `normal` included** — treating real content as empty erases a boundary, treating a sparse chart as content only keeps one; **when in doubt keep the boundary**. **Content, not workflow status** — 29 of 32 production rows are `finalized`, so a status shortcut would have been wrong.

**PostgreSQL portability (load-bearing).** `tooth_map_payload` is `jsonb`; comparing it to a string is a hard PG error (no `jsonb = text` operator) that SQLite tolerates — it would pass every local test and 500 in production. Only `whereNotNull` is portable in SQL; `{"teeth": {}}` and a missing `teeth` key are settled in PHP. **`whereJsonLength()` is forbidden here** — it compiles to `jsonb_array_length()` on PG, which errors on a JSON object.

**Test-fixture trap.** `OdontogramFactory` defaults `tooth_map_payload` to `NULL`, so `lodoNativeOdontogram()` had been manufacturing exactly the placeholder the cutoff must ignore — every pre-existing cutoff test asserted against a row with no chart. The helper now charts a tooth in the real production shape; new `lodoEmptyNativeOdontogram()` covers the contentless case. Tests: new `tests/Feature/LegacyOdontogram/LegacyOdontogramNativeReferenceCutoffTest.php` (19). **Negative control: 9 of 19 failed before the fix, 10 already passed** (the meaningful-data protections). `tests/Feature/LegacyOdontogram/*` already reaches CI via the `LegacyOdontogram` alternative in the `--filter=` of both critical-gate variants — no workflow change needed (verified, not assumed).

## TEST-BASELINE-MONITORING-FAILURES-1 — the Monitoring baseline is zero, and it is deterministic (2026-08-22)

Branch `feature/test-baseline-monitoring-failures-1` (base `feature/sprint-26-phase-26-8-stabilization-closure-go-watch-no-go-report` @ `6b56ad9`, peeled `legacy-odontogram-native-reference-cutoff-1-go`; do NOT target main). Sprint doc `docs/sprints/test-baseline-monitoring-failures-1.md`; rule mirror `.cursor/rules/112-monitoring-baseline-zero-known-failures.mdc`. **No migration, no route, no permission, no schema, no clinical-workflow change.**
